The Bronze Statue states that Xiaochangshu (BL 27) treats dark-reddish urine, difficult/astringent urination, stranguria (with a textual corruption '3254'), and lower abdominal pain; Qianjin Yaofang also states it treats lower abdominal distension and fullness. It is a key point for lower abdominal distension and pain, and other points are moxibusted only if moxibustion here proves ineffective.

💡 术语：『小便赤澁』指小便短赤、排尿涩滞不畅；『淋』即淋证，指小便频急、滴沥刺痛。『3254』为底本缺字，本书“淋3254”“遗3254”多处出现，当为“沥”。医理：小肠俞（BL 27）为小肠背俞穴，能疏调下焦气机、分清泌浊。涉及穴位：小肠俞（BL 27）。现代理解与对照：小肠俞位于骶区，对应骶神经后支及盆腔内脏神经节段调控区，对膀胱逼尿肌功能、排尿反射及盆腔内脏痛有一定调节作用；临床上常作为泌尿生殖与下腹痛的配穴或辅助疗法，但严重泌尿系感染、结石或梗阻需优先现代医学检查与抗感染/排石治疗。

㿗疝諸疝氣胎疝寒疝卒疝

【㿗疝、诸疝气、胎疝、寒疝、卒疝】

[Tui Hernia, Various Hernia Qi Disorders, Fetal Hernia, Cold Hernia, and Sudden Hernia]

💡 术语：『㿗疝』多指阴囊肿大坠胀或疝气；『诸疝气』泛指各类疝气及腹股沟、阴囊、少腹坠胀疼痛类病证；『胎疝』指先天性或小儿出生即见的疝气；『寒疝』指寒邪凝滞少腹、阴囊引起的冷痛拘急；『卒疝』指突发剧烈的疝痛或少腹绞痛。现代理解与对照：古代『疝』涵盖范围广，既包括腹股沟疝、鞘膜积液、睾丸/精索病变，也包含部分肠痉挛、泌尿系结石绞痛或盆腔痛。临床遇急腹症或嵌顿疝须警惕外科急症，切忌盲目艾灸延误手术时机。

必用方云治水㿗偏大上下不定疼不可忍俗呼為膀胱氣用煅過牡礪二兩炮乾薑一兩為末塗病處即愈則是水㿗即膀胱氣也千金云氣衝主㿗明堂下經云治㿉疝則是㿗即㿉疝也恐人惑其名而悞治之故為之辯

《必用方》说治疗水㿗——阴囊偏大、时而上缩时而下坠、疼痛难忍，俗称『膀胱气』——用煅过的牡蛎二两、炮制过的干姜一两研成细末，敷在患处就能痊愈；由此可知水㿗就是膀胱气。《千金要方》说气冲主治㿗病，《明堂下经》说治疗㿉疝，由此可知『㿗』就是『㿉疝』。王执中担心人们被这些病名混淆而误治，所以特此辨析。

Biyong Fang states that to treat water tui—marked by unilateral enlargement of the scrotum, shifting up and down, and unbearable pain, commonly called 'bladder qi'—one grinds two liang of calcined oyster shell and one liang of prepared (pao) dried ginger into a fine powder and applies it topically to the affected area for recovery; thus water tui is bladder qi. Qianjin Yaofang states that Qichong (ST 30) treats tui, while Mingtang Xiajing states that it treats tui hernia; thus 'tui' is 'tui hernia.' Fearing people might be confused by the names and mistreat the condition, the author clarifies this distinction.

💡 术语：『水㿗』指阴囊积液或水肿样偏大；『膀胱气』是民间俗称，指下焦气滞或寒湿下注所致少腹及阴囊坠痛；『煅』指高温煅烧以增强收涩制酸、燥湿敛疮作用；『炮干姜』指炮制后的干姜，增强温经散寒之力；『明堂下经』为古针灸文献名。医理与辨析：古人常将阴囊肿痛归于『㿗』『㿉疝』『膀胱气』等名目，实为同源异名或同类病证的不同表现。涉及穴位：气冲（ST 30）。现代理解与对照：阴囊肿大伴疼痛或移动性包块，在现代医学中可对应睾丸鞘膜积液、腹股沟斜疝、精索静脉曲张或附睾睾丸炎等。外敷药粉在古代属于局部消肿止痛外治法，但现代临床若怀疑嵌顿疝、睾丸扭转或急性感染，必须及时就医排查，不可仅靠外敷或针灸。

曲泉主㿉疝隂跳痛引臍中千中都合陽中郄關元大巨交信中封太衝地機主㿗疝中封主㿗疝癃暴痛痿厥少府主隂痛實時挺長寒熱隂暴痛遺尿偏虛則暴癢氣逆卒疝小便不利衝門主婦人隂疝商丘主隂股内痛氣癰狐疝走上下引小腹痛不可俛仰巨闕主狐疝太衝主狐疝歐厥肩井旁肩解與臂相接處主偏㿗氣衝主㿗隂腫

曲泉主治㿉疝、阴部跳痛牵引到肚脐中央（出自《千金》）；中都、合阳、中郄（委中的别名）、关元、大巨、交信、中封、太冲、地机都主治㿗疝。中封主治㿗疝、小便不利、突然剧痛、痿软厥冷。少府主治阴部疼痛，邪实时阴茎挺长、寒热交作，阴部突然剧痛、遗尿，偏虚时则突然发痒、气逆、突发疝痛、小便不利。冲门主治妇女阴疝。商丘主治阴股内侧痛、气痈、狐疝——狐疝的特征是包块上下游走、牵引小腹痛得不能俯仰。巨阙主治狐疝。太冲主治狐疝、呕吐厥逆。肩井旁、肩解与臂相接处主治偏㿗。气冲主治㿗病、阴囊肿痛。

Ququan (LR 8) treats tui hernia and throbbing pain in the genitalia pulling toward the center of the umbilicus (from Qianjin); Zhongdu (LR 6), Heyang (BL 55), Zhongxi (likely a textual error), Guanyuan (CV 4), Daju (ST 27), Jiaoxin (KI 8), Zhongfeng (LR 4), Taichong (LR 3), and Diji (SP 8) all treat tui hernia. Zhongfeng (LR 4) treats tui hernia, urinary retention/difficulty, sudden severe pain, flaccidity, and cold limbs (jue). Shaofu (HT 8) treats genital pain: in excess states, erection/elongation of the penis with chills and fever, sudden severe genital pain, and enuresis; in deficient states, sudden itching, counterflow qi, sudden hernia, and difficult urination. Chongmen (SP 12) treats female inguinal/genital hernia. Shangqiu (SP 5) treats inner thigh pain, qi carbuncle, and fox hernia (hu shan), which moves up and down and pulls the lower abdomen with pain preventing bending forward or backward. Juque (CV 14) treats fox hernia. Taichong (LR 3) treats fox hernia, vomiting, and cold/syncope (jue). The area beside Jianjing (GB 21) where the shoulder joint meets the arm treats unilateral tui. Qichong (ST 30) treats tui and scrotal swelling.

💡 术语：『阴跳痛引脐中』指阴囊或会阴部搏动性疼痛牵引脐周；『痿厥』指肢体痿软无力或四肢厥冷；『狐疝』指疝气如狐狸般昼出夜入、随卧立或体位变化上下移动，多见于可复性腹股沟疝；『气痈』指气滞痰凝或湿热蕴结所致局部肿痛包块；『肩解』指肩关节部位。医理：足厥阴肝经绕阴器、抵少腹，足太阴脾经络阴器、循股内，足少阴肾经贯脊属肾络膀胱，故多选用肝、脾、肾经及任脉、胃经穴位调理下焦气血。涉及穴位：曲泉（LR 8）、中都（LR 6）、合阳（BL 55）、中郄（即委中 BL 40）、关元（CV 4）、大巨（ST 27）、交信（KI 8）、中封（LR 4）、太冲（LR 3）、地机（SP 8）、少府（HT 8）、冲门（SP 12）、商丘（SP 5）、巨阙（CV 14）、肩井（GB 21）、气冲（ST 30）。现代理解与对照：『狐疝』与可复性腹股沟疝特征高度吻合，现代医学认为可复性疝若反复发作或有嵌顿风险，修补术是根治手段；针灸仅可作为缓解局部气滞疼痛或术后康复的辅助措施，不可替代外科评估。

痛中管主衝疝冒死不知人交信主氣癃㿗疝隂急股樞膊内廉痛臍中石門天樞氣海主小腹疝氣遊行五藏疝繞臍衝胷不得息並千臍疝繞臍痛衝胷不得息灸臍中臍疝繞臍痛石門主之臍疝繞臍痛時止天樞主之又主氣疝煩嘔千金主氣疝嘔靣腫賁豚並甲氣衝主㿗明下作㿉疝隂腫痛隂痿莖中痛兩丸騫痛不可仰卧五樞主隂疝兩丸上入小腹痛明下云主隂疝小腹痛隂交石門太衝主兩丸騫見隂縮交信見淋中都見腸鳴大巨曲骨見小腹治㿉疝銅曲泉治大夫㿉疝隂股痛小便難腹脅支滿癃閉少氣洩利四支不舉實即身熱目3392痛汗不出目䀮䀮膝痛筋攣不可屈伸千金曰㿗有四種腸㿗卵脹難灸氣㿗水㿗鍼灸易治卵偏大上入腹灸三隂交隨年卵偏大㿗病灸關元百壮或大敦隨年壮或横骨邊二七壮夾莖是詳見千金築賓治小兒胎疝明下同痛不得乳小兒胎疝卵偏重灸囊後縫十字文當上三壮春較夏灸秋較冬灸太衝主女子疝及小腹腫溏泄癃遺尿隂痛靣黑目皆痛漏血千蠡溝主女子疝赤白淫下時多時少暴腹痛隂交石門主疝見無子小兒氣㿗灸足厥隂大敦左灸右右灸左各一壮太倉公診司空命婦曰疝氣客于膀胱難于前後溲而溺赤灸其足蹶隂脉左右各一所即不遺溺而溲清更為火齊湯飲之而疝氣散隂市肝俞療寒疝下至腰脚如冷水水傷諸疝按之在膝上伏兎下寒痛腹脹滿厥少氣明下云卒疝小腹痛力痿氣少伏兎中寒腰如冷水銅云寒疝小腹脹腰以下伏兎上寒如冷水合陽治寒疝隂偏痛銅然谷治寒疝小腹脹上搶胷脇次髎治疝氣下墜腰脊痛不得轉摇急引隂器痛不可忍腰下至足不仁背腠寒小便赤淋心下堅脹大谿行間見白濁肓俞見腹脹肝俞治寒疝見欬明同隂交治寒

疼痛。中管（即中脘）主治冲疝、昏厥如死、不省人事。交信主治气癃、㿗疝、阴部拘急、股枢及小腿内侧痛（原文“膊”疑为“腨”）。脐中、石门、天枢、气海主治小腹疝气游走、五脏疝、绕脐痛且气冲胸口导致呼吸不得。脐疝绕脐痛、气冲胸口呼吸不得，可艾灸脐中。脐疝绕脐痛，石门主治。脐疝绕脐痛、时作时止，天枢主治，天枢又主治气疝、烦闷呕吐。《千金要方》主治气疝、呕吐、面部浮肿、贲豚（并见《甲乙经》）。气冲主治㿗病，《明堂下经》作『㿉疝』，伴有阴囊肿痛、阴痿、尿道中痛、两侧睾丸上缩牵引疼痛、不能仰卧。五枢主治阴疝、两侧睾丸上入小腹疼痛；《明堂下经》说主治阴疝、小腹痛。阴交、石门、太冲主治两侧睾丸上缩（详见『阴缩』条）。交信见『淋』条，中都见『肠鸣』条，大巨、曲骨见『小腹』条；《铜人》说曲泉治疗男子㿉疝、阴股痛、小便难、腹胁胀满、癃闭、少气、腹泻、四肢不能举动，邪实时则身热、目眩痛（3392为底本缺字，当为“眩”）、无汗、视物昏花、膝痛、筋挛不能屈伸。《千金》说㿗有四种：肠㿗、卵胀（难以艾灸）、气㿗、水㿗（针灸容易治疗）。睾丸偏大并向上入腹，按年龄壮数艾灸三阴交；睾丸偏大患㿗病，艾灸关元百壮，或随年龄壮数艾灸大敦，或在横骨旁两侧各灸二七壮（夹住阴茎两侧），详见《千金》。筑宾主治小儿胎疝（《明堂下经》同），痛得不能吃奶。小儿胎疝、睾丸偏重，在阴囊后正中缝十字纹正上方艾灸三壮，春天灸，到夏天就好；秋天灸，到冬天就好。太冲主治女子疝及小腹肿、大便溏泄、小便不利、遗尿、阴部疼痛、面色发黑、目眦痛、崩漏出血（出《千金》）。蠡沟主治女子疝、赤白带下时多时少、突发腹痛。阴交、石门主治疝（见『无子』条）。小儿气㿗，艾灸足厥阴大敦穴，左病灸右、右病灸左，各灸一壮。太仓公（淳于意）诊断司空命妇说：疝气寄居在膀胱，大小便困难且小便赤红，艾灸她足厥阴经脉左右各一处，随即不再遗尿且小便清澈，再配制火齐汤给她饮用，疝气就散了。阴市、肝俞治疗寒疝，寒气下行至腰脚如冷水一般。水伤诸疝，按压时在膝上、伏兔下有寒痛、腹胀满、厥冷、少气；《明堂下经》说主治卒疝、小腹痛、肢体痿软、气短，伏兔部位发寒、腰如冷水。《铜人》说寒疝小腹胀、腰以下至伏兔以上寒冷如冷水。合阳治疗寒疝、阴部偏痛（出《铜人》）。然谷治疗寒疝、小腹胀满、气上冲胸胁。次髎治疗疝气下坠、腰脊痛得不能转动、急迫牵引阴器痛不可忍、腰以下至足麻木、背部腠理发寒、小便赤红如淋、心下坚硬胀满。大溪、行间见『白浊』条，肓俞见『腹胀』条，肝俞治寒疝见『欬』条（《明堂下经》同）。阴交治疗寒

Jiaoxin (KI 8) treats qi urinary retention/difficulty, tui hernia, genital tension/spasm, and pain in the hip joint (gushu) and inner upper arm. Shenque (CV 8), Shimen (CV 5), Tianshu (ST 25), and Qihai (CV 6) treat lower abdominal hernia qi moving around, five-zang hernia, and periumbilical pain rushing into the chest and causing shortness of breath (all from Qianjin). For umbilical hernia with periumbilical pain rushing into the chest and causing dyspnea, moxibustion is applied to Shenque (CV 8). Shimen (CV 5) treats umbilical hernia with periumbilical pain. Tianshu (ST 25) treats umbilical hernia with intermittent periumbilical pain, and also treats qi hernia with vexation and vomiting. Qianjin treats qi hernia, vomiting, facial edema, and running piglet (bentun) (together with Jiayi Jing). Qichong (ST 30) treats tui, rendered in Mingtang Xiajing as 'tui hernia' with scrotal swelling and pain, impotence, pain inside the penis, and upward retraction of both testes with pain preventing lying supine. Wushu (GB 27) treats yin hernia with both testes ascending into the lower abdomen and causing pain; Mingtang Xiajing states it treats yin hernia and lower abdominal pain. Yinjiao (CV 7), Shimen (CV 5), and Taichong (LR 3) treat upward retraction of both testes (see 'Genital Retraction'). Jiaoxin (KI 8) is covered under 'Stranguria,' Zhongdu (LR 6) under 'Borborygmus,' and Daju (ST 27) and Qugu (CV 2) under 'Lower Abdomen'; Tongren states Ququan (LR 8) treats male tui hernia, inner thigh pain, difficult urination, abdominal and hypochondriac distension and fullness, urinary retention/difficulty, qi deficiency, diarrhea, and inability to lift the four limbs, and in excess states fever, dizzy, painful eyes, anhidrosis, blurred vision, knee pain, and tendon spasm preventing flexion or extension. Qianjin states there are four types of tui: intestinal tui and testicular distension (hard to treat with moxibustion), and qi tui and water tui (easily treated with acupuncture and moxibustion). For a testicle unilaterally enlarged and ascending into the abdomen, moxibust Sanyinjiao (SP 6) with a number of cones equal to the patient's age; for a unilaterally enlarged testicle with tui disease, moxibust Guanyuan (CV 4) for 100 cones, or Dadun (LR 1) with cones equal to age, or two sets of seven cones on each side of Henggu (CV 2) flanking the penis, detailed in Qianjin. Zhubin (KI 9) treats infantile fetal hernia (same in Mingtang Xiajing) with pain preventing nursing. For infantile fetal hernia with a heavy/enlarged testicle on one side, moxibust three cones directly above the cross-shaped crease on the posterior midline of the scrotum (raphe), moxibustion in spring brings recovery by summer, in autumn by winter. Taichong (LR 3) treats female hernia, lower abdominal swelling, loose stools, urinary retention/difficulty, enuresis, genital pain, dark complexion, outer canthus pain, and metrorrhagia/metrostaxis (from Qianjin). Ligou (LR 5) treats female hernia, intermittent red and white vaginal discharge, and sudden abdominal pain. Yinjiao (CV 7) and Shimen (CV 5) treat hernia as seen under 'Infertility.' For infantile qi tui, moxibust Foot Jueyin Dadun (LR 1)—left side for right-sided disease and right side for left-sided disease—one cone each. Grand Physician Cang Gong (Chunyu Yi) diagnosed the wife of the Minister of Works (Sikong), saying hernia qi lodged in the bladder causes difficulty in urination and defecation with red urine; moxibusting her Foot Jueyin channel once on the left and once on the right immediately stopped enuresis and cleared the urine, and administering Huoqi Decoction dispersed the hernia qi. Yinshi (ST 31) and Ganshu (BL 18) treat cold hernia descending to the waist and feet like cold water. Water-injured hernias present on palpation with cold pain above the knee and below Futu (ST 32), abdominal distension and fullness, cold limbs (jue), and qi deficiency; Mingtang Xiajing states it treats sudden hernia, lower abdominal pain, limb weakness, and qi deficiency, with coldness in Futu (ST 32) and the waist like cold water. Tongren states cold hernia causes lower abdominal distension and coldness from the waist down to above Futu (ST 32) like cold water. Heyang (BL 55) treats cold hernia with unilateral genital pain (from Tongren). Rangu (KI 2) treats cold hernia with lower abdominal distension and qi rushing up into the chest and hypochondrium. Ciliao (BL 32) treats hernia dragging downward, lumbospinal pain preventing turning, acute pulling pain in the genitalia/groin that is unbearable, numbness from the waist down to the feet, coldness of the back pores/skin, reddish urine like stranguria, and hard distension below the heart. Daxi (KI 3) and Xingjian (LR 2) are covered under 'Turbid Urine,' Huangshu (KI 16) under 'Abdominal Distension,' and Ganshu (BL 18) for cold hernia under 'Cough' (same in Mingtang Xiajing). 💡 本条汇集各书治疝的穴位。涉及穴位：中脘 CV12（原文“中管”）、交信 KI8、神阙 CV8（脐中）、石门 CV5、天枢 ST25、气海 CV6、气冲 ST30、五枢 GB27、阴交 CV7、太冲 LR3、中都 LR6、大巨 ST27、曲骨 CV2、曲泉 LR8、三阴交 SP6、关元 CV4、大敦 LR1、横骨 KI11、筑宾 KI9、蠡沟 LR5、阴市 ST33、肝俞 BL18、合阳 BL55、然谷 KI2、次髎 BL32、太溪 KI3、行间 LR2、肓俞 KI16。“㿗”“㿉疝”指阴囊肿大坠痛一类，“卵偏大”多见于腹股沟斜疝、鞘膜积液；“冲疝”是气从少腹上冲的疝痛。太仓公（淳于意）为司空命妇灸足厥阴脉的医案出自《史记·扁鹊仓公列传》。现代遇到阴囊突然剧痛或肿块不能回纳，要先排除睾丸扭转、嵌顿疝，属外科急症；文中五百壮、百壮等灸量与“春灸夏瘥”的说法只作文献理解，不能照搬。

疝引小腹痛腰膝拘攣五樞治男子寒疝卵上入小腹痛中封治寒疝引腰中痛或身微熱大敦主寒疝隂挺出見下

疝气牵引小腹痛、腰膝拘挛。五枢主治男子寒疝、睾丸上入小腹疼痛。中封主治寒疝牵引腰中疼痛，或伴有轻微发热。大敦主治寒疝、阴茎挺出（详见下文）。

Zhongfeng (LR 4) treats cold hernia pulling into the mid-lumbar region with pain, or accompanied by slight fever. Dadun (LR 1) treats cold hernia and protrusion/erection of the penis (see below).

💡 术语：『腰膝拘挛』指腰部与膝部筋脉拘急、屈伸不利；『阴挺出』指阴茎异常挺出或阴器下坠突出。医理：肝脉循股阴、入毛中、过阴器、抵小腹，胆脉行身侧、络少腹，寒邪客于厥阴、少阳或下焦则见拘挛、引痛及阴器异常。涉及穴位：五枢（GB 27）、中封（LR 4）、大敦（LR 1）。现代理解与对照：下腹、腹股沟及腰膝放射痛在现代医学中可涉及腹股沟区神经受压、腰椎病变、精索/睾丸牵涉痛或盆底肌痉挛；穴位刺激对缓解局部肌肉痉挛及神经性疼痛有一定辅助作用。

舎弟少戲舉重得偏墜之疾有客人為當關元兩旁相去各三寸青脉上灸七壮即愈王彦賓患小腸氣亦如此灸之愈餘見膀胱

我的弟弟年少嬉戏时举重物，得了『偏坠』的病；有一位客人为他在关元穴两旁各相距三寸、有青筋的地方艾灸七壮，随即痊愈。王彦宾患『小肠气』，也像这样艾灸而痊愈。其余内容见『膀胱』条。

My younger brother, while playing and lifting heavy objects in his youth, developed 'unilateral descent' (pianzui); a guest moxibusted seven cones over visible bluish veins three cun to each side of Guanyuan (CV 4), and he recovered immediately. Wang Yanbin suffered from 'small intestine qi' and was likewise cured by moxibustion in this manner. Remaining details are found under 'Bladder.'

💡 术语：『偏坠』指阴囊或腹股沟一侧下坠肿大；『小肠气』指脐周或少腹牵引阴囊坠胀疼痛的疝气类病证；『青脉』指皮下浅表静脉显露（青紫或青色脉络）；『壮』是艾灸计量单位。医理：因举重用力过度、气机下陷或血络瘀滞导致偏坠，在关元旁开三寸、有青筋处施灸（此处比胃经水道旁开2寸更靠外，不是标准穴位），借温热通经活络、升提下陷之气。涉及穴位：关元（CV 4）（旁开三寸处）。现代理解与对照：腹压骤增（如举重）诱发腹股沟疝或精索静脉曲张是常见临床诱因；在浅表静脉上直接艾灸需注意避免烫伤静脉壁或引发局部皮下出血/血栓性静脉炎，现代更倾向在安全穴位施灸或由外科评估疝气。

金门见『尸厥』条；丘墟见『腋肿』条，又主治突发疝痛。大敦主治卒疝、小便频数、遗尿、尿道口（阴头中）痛、心痛、出汗、阴器上缩入腹、阴囊偏大、腹痛及脐中痛、心情郁闷不乐；病在左侧取右侧穴，病在右侧取左侧穴。蠡沟主治卒疝、小腹肿、有时小腹突然剧痛、小便不利如癃闭、频频嗳气、恐惧心悸、少气不足、腹痛、心情郁闷不乐、咽喉中发闷好像有息肉、背部拘急不能俯仰。太冲主治小儿卒疝、呕吐、发冷、咽干、足背或踝部浮肿（胕肿）、内踝前痛、四肢倦怠酸软（淫泺）、小腿酸痛、腋下肿；《明堂下经》说治疗卒疝、小腹痛、小便不利像淋病一样。照海主治卒疝、小腹痛、呕吐、嗜睡。阴跷治疗卒疝、小腹痛（症状同上），左病取右、右病取左，可立即止痛（《明堂下经》同）。蠡沟治疗卒疝、小腿或大腿内侧肿（小股肿）、小便不利（交仪是蠡沟的别名，所以说“交仪同”），以及脐下有积气如卵石、足冷、小腿酸痛、屈伸困难（见下文）。石门治疗卒疝、绕脐痛。关元治疗卒疝（《铜人》作『暴疝』，《千金》同）、小腹痛、转胞不得小便。陷谷治疗卒疝、小腹痛。交信见『淋』条。交信也治卒疝。华池（所指不明）治突然阴卵偏大：取足大趾去指甲五分、内侧赤白肉际处艾灸三壮，艾炷如半个枣核大，左病取右、右病取左。照海主治四肢倦怠无力、全身闷胀、阴部突然发作疝气（出《千金》）。大敦主治卒疝暴痛、阴部跳痛上入腹、寒疝、阴茎挺出、阴囊偏大肿胀、脐腹间心情郁闷不乐、小便困难而疼痛，灸刺可立即缓解，左病取右、右病取左；《甲乙经》说照海主治此证。

💡 术语：『左取右，病右取左』即巨刺/缪刺交叉取穴法，通过经络对侧呼应缓解急痛；『转胞』指膀胱受压或气机阻滞所致小便不通；『赤白肉际』指手足掌跖部皮肤色泽深浅交界处；『华池』在古籍中多指口津或特定旁注误入。医理：急骤发作的少腹、阴部绞痛多属肝经气滞寒凝、气机逆乱，取井穴（大敦）、输穴（太冲）、八脉交会穴（照海，通阴跷脉）及络穴（蠡沟）可疏肝理气、通调下焦。涉及穴位：金门（BL 63）、丘墟（GB 40）、大敦（LR 1）、蠡沟（LR 5）、太冲（LR 3）、照海（KI 6）、石门（CV 5）、关元（CV 4）、陷谷（ST 43）、交信（KI 8）。现代理解与对照：突发剧烈少腹或阴囊痛伴恶心呕吐，在现代医学中需排查嵌顿性腹股沟疝、睾丸扭转、输尿管结石肾绞痛等急症；跨侧取穴在针灸镇痛中有一定临床经验价值，但急腹症须先排除器质性急危重症。

💡 术语：『胞闭塞』指下焦气机闭阻或胞宫、膀胱气化不利；『石淋』指尿中有砂石或排尿涩痛伴砂石排出；『五淋』指气淋、血淋、膏淋、劳淋、热淋五种淋证；『气门』是《千金方》所载的奇穴，在关元旁开三寸。医理：关元为任脉穴、小肠募穴，培补下元、助膀胱气化；大敦疏肝泄热，气门为局部取穴；悬钟为髓会，通调经气。涉及穴位：关元（CV 4）、悬钟（GB 39）、大敦（LR 1）、气冲（ST 30）。现代理解与对照：『石淋』与泌尿系结石（肾/输尿管/膀胱结石）相近，『热淋』『五淋』常见于尿路感染、乳糜尿、前列腺炎等。针灸对部分功能性排尿障碍或结石所致疼痛可起辅助作用，但结石梗阻或急性尿潴留须及时现代医学干预以防肾功能受损。

💡 术语：『小便如散火』形容小便赤热、灼痛如火星四散；『隔蒜/隔盐灸』之『盐着脐中灸』即隔盐灸，借盐吸湿、助热透达下焦；『鼠鼷』指腹股沟部位；『不觉遗』指不知不觉遗尿。医理：分型施治体现中医辨证论治思想——热淋、血淋清利湿热或凉血止血，气淋理气行滞，劳淋健脾益肾温阳。涉及穴位：照海（KI 6）、石门（CV 5）、长强（GV 1）、曲骨（CV 2）、至阴（BL 67）、中极（CV 3）、复溜（KI 7）、次髎（BL 32）、然谷（KI 2）、太冲（LR 3）、阴陵泉（SP 9）、交信（KI 8）、箕门（SP 11）、大钟（KI 4）、关元（CV 4）、大敦（LR 1）、中封（LR 4）、水泉（KI 5）、委阳（BL 39）、志室（BL 52）、中髎（BL 33）、小肠俞（BL 27）。现代理解与对照：尿频尿痛、血尿、排尿困难及尿失禁在现代医学中涉及泌尿道感染、结石、前列腺疾病、神经源性膀胱等；注意『隔盐灸』在肚脐操作时需控制艾炷大小与温度，避免烫伤脐部皮肤或引起感染，血尿及尿潴留应及时就医检查。

予壮年寓學忽有遺3254之患因閲方書見有用五倍子末酒調服者服之而愈藥若相投豈在多品而亦無事於灸也故附著于此若欲治淋疾則有王不留行子神效彭侍郎以治張道士服三粒愈見既效方有婦人患淋卧病久之服諸藥愈甚其夫入夜來告急予令取此花葉十餘葉令研細煎服翌朝再來云病已減八分再與數葉煎服即愈一名剪金花一名金盞銀臺

我壮年在外求学时，突然患了小便遗沥（尿后滴沥不尽；原文“遗3254”，3254为底本缺字，当为“沥”），因为翻阅医书看到有用五倍子粉末用酒调服的方法，服用后就痊愈了。药物如果对症，哪里在于品味多呢？而且也用不着艾灸了，所以把它附录在这里。如果想要治疗淋病，则有王不留行子非常神效，彭侍郎用它来治疗张道士，服下三粒就痊愈了（见《既效方》）。有一位妇人患淋病，卧床病了很久，服用了各种药物反而更重；她的丈夫深夜来告急，我让他们取这种花（即王不留行）的叶子十多片，研细后煎水服用，第二天早上再来报告说病情已经减轻了八分，再给几片叶子煎服就痊愈了。它又叫『剪金花』，又叫『金盏银台』。

When I was studying away from home in my prime, I suddenly suffered from urinary dribbling (yili); reading medical books, I found a method using fine powder of Galla Chinensis (wubeizi) mixed with wine, took it, and recovered. If a remedy matches the pattern, why would it require many ingredients, nor would moxibustion be needed—so I append it here. If one wishes to treat stranguria, seeds of Vaccaria segetalis (wangbuliuxingzi) are remarkably effective; Vice Minister Peng used it to treat Daoist Master Zhang, who recovered after taking three seeds (see Jixiao Fang). A woman suffered from stranguria and lay ill in bed for a long time, worsening despite taking various medications; her husband came urgently at night, and I had them take a dozen or so leaves of this flowering plant, grind them fine, and decoct them for oral administration. The next morning he returned saying the illness had already subsided by eighty percent, and after taking a few more leaves decocted, she recovered. Another name is 'Jianjinhua' (Scissors Gold Flower) and another is 'Jinzhan Yintai' (Golden Cup and Silver Platter).

💡 术语：『遗3254』即遗沥，小便滴沥不尽；『五倍子』性酸涩收敛，能固精止遗；『王不留行』性平苦，善行血通淋下乳；宋代《本草图经》记王不留行俗名剪金花、金盏银台，与后世叫金盏银台的水仙不是一物。医理：王执中虽为针灸医家，但不拘泥于针灸，主张『药若相投，岂在多品』，针药并用、随证施治。涉及穴位：本段主要为内服中药医案与经验方，未列特定针灸穴位。现代理解与对照：五倍子富含鞣质，具收敛作用，但大剂量或长期内服可能影响胃肠及肝肾功能，且含酒精调服对泌尿道刺激或肝病患者不宜；王不留行在现代中医临床常用于乳汁不通、痛经、淋证等，但草药同名异物现象明显，临床用药需经药师鉴别炮制，泌尿系感染仍应规范评估。

小便難不通不利

【小便难、不通、不利】

[Difficult Urination, Urinary Blockage, and Unhindered/Smooth Voiding Impairment]

💡 术语：『小便难』指排尿费力、困难；『不通』指尿液闭塞排出障碍；『不利』指排尿不畅、涩滞或量少。现代理解与对照：对应排尿困难、尿流无力、尿潴留及下尿路症状（LUTS），需结合病史区分梗阻性、感染性或神经功能性原因。

💡 术语：『阴下纵』指阴囊或阴茎下垂弛缓；『振寒』指寒战发抖；『溲白』指小便浑浊发白（如乳糜尿、脓尿或寒湿之象）；『烈缺』即肺经络穴列缺（LU 7）；『屈骨端』即曲骨（CV 2）。医理：任脉及肝、脾、肾、膀胱经循行少腹及前阴，配伍远端经穴（涌泉、行间、中封、列缺、足三里等）可宣通气机、升清降浊、利水通淋。涉及穴位：涌泉（KI 1）、曲骨（CV 2）、曲泉（LR 8）、阴交（CV 7）、石门（CV 5）、委阳（BL 39）、关元（CV 4）、气冲（ST 30）、大敦（LR 1）、照海（KI 6）、横骨（KI 11）、大巨（ST 27）、期门（LR 14）、阴谷（KI 10）、箕门（SP 11）、委中（BL 40）、中封（LR 4）、行间（LR 2）、列缺（LU 7）、少府（HT 8）、足三里（ST 36）、阴陵泉（SP 9）。现代理解与对照：急性尿潴留属于泌尿外科急症，若膀胱高度充盈无法排尿，应及时导尿或就医处理，避免膀胱过度膨胀或肾积水；慢性排尿不畅或尿路刺激症状可作为针灸辅助调理的适应证之一。

💡 术语：『小便五色』指观察小便青、赤、黄、白、黑五种色泽变化以诊察脏腑寒热虚实与病位。现代理解与对照：尿液颜色受水分摄入、饮食、药物、胆红素、血红蛋白或肌红蛋白、泌尿道感染等多种因素影响；肉眼血尿、深茶色尿或持续异常尿色应做尿常规及必要影像检查。

💡 术语：『小便如血』即尿血；『溺海』在古籍中多为膀胱或下焦水液聚散之误写/借代；『兑端』为督脉穴（GV 27，上唇尖端），与龈交（GV 28）不是同一穴。医理：尿血或赤涩多与热扰下焦、阴虚火旺或气血失调有关，常取膀胱经背俞穴（膀胱俞、小肠俞、白环俞、秩边、阳纲、魂门）及任脉、肾经穴位调理。涉及穴位：关元（CV 4）、大陵（PC 7）、下脘（CV 10）、阴交（CV 7）、照海（KI 6）、小肠俞（BL 27）、大溪（KI 3）、白环俞（BL 30）、膀胱俞（BL 28）、上廉（LI 9）、兑端（GV 27）、阴谷（KI 10）、下廉（LI 8）、魂门（BL 47）、秩边（BL 54）、气海（CV 6）、阳纲（BL 48）。现代理解与对照：尿血是现代医学的重要警示征象，可由泌尿系结石、感染、肾小球疾病、前列腺增生或泌尿系肿瘤引起；临床必须先做尿液分析及泌尿系统检查明确病因，切忌仅靠针灸延误诊治。

treats reddish urine is covered under 'Abdominal Pain.' Dadun (LR 1) treats hematuria, moxibusted for three cones (from Qianjin).

💡 术语：『大敦』为足厥阴肝经井穴，五行属木，位于足大趾末节外侧，距趾甲角0.1寸。医理：肝主疏泄、藏血，厥阴经脉绕阴器、抵少腹，以井穴大敦艾灸三壮调理下焦气血。涉及穴位：大敦（LR 1）。现代理解与对照：现代医学中出现尿血首先应排查器质性出血原因（如结石、感染、肿瘤等），在明确诊断并排除禁忌后，穴位疗法仅作辅助，不作止血首选。

小便有五色惟赤白色者多赤色多因酒得之宜服本事方清心丸予敎人服効白色乃下元冷宜服補藥著灸腎俞關元小腸俞膀胱俞等皆要穴也近有患小便出血者人敎酒與水煎苦蕒菜根服即愈

小便虽然有五种颜色，但唯独赤色和白色最常见。小便赤色大多是因为饮酒所致，适宜服用《本事方》的『清心丸』，我教人服用很有效。小便白色则是下元虚寒，适宜服用温补药，并施灸于肾俞、关元、小肠俞、膀胱俞等穴位，这些都是要穴。近来有人患小便出血，旁人教他用酒和水煎煮苦荬菜根服用，随即痊愈。

Although urine has five colors, reddish and whitish are the most common. Reddish urine is mostly caused by drinking alcohol and is suitably treated with Clear the Heart Pill (Qingxin Wan) from Benshi Fang, which I have taught others to take with good effect. Whitish urine indicates coldness in the lower origin (xiayuan), so tonifying medicinal formulas should be taken together with moxibustion at Shenshu (BL 23), Guanyuan (CV 4), Xiaochangshu (BL 27), and Pangguangshu (BL 28), all of which are key points. Recently, someone suffering from hematuria was advised by another to decoct the root of sow thistle/bitter lettuce (kumaicai) in wine and water and drink it, recovering immediately.

💡 术语：『下元冷』指肾阳或命门真阳虚衰、下焦虚寒；『苦荬菜』即菊科苦菜/苦苣菜类草本植物，性味苦寒，清热凉血。医理：饮酒助湿热、扰心营下移可致尿赤；下元虚寒则气化失司、尿液清冷或呈白浊，故内服补益与背俞、关元温灸并重。涉及穴位：肾俞（BL 23）、关元（CV 4）、小肠俞（BL 27）、膀胱俞（BL 28）。现代理解与对照：饮酒后尿色加深或浑浊常与脱水、酒精代谢影响或泌尿道刺激有关；尿液呈白色/乳白色在现代医学中可对应乳糜尿、脓尿或磷酸盐结晶尿；血尿若用酒煎草药，酒精可能加重黏膜刺激或增加出血风险，临床遇血尿应优先做尿常规与影像学检查。

治夢遺失精白濁

【治梦遗、失精、白浊】

[Treatment of Nocturnal Emission (Mengyi), Seminal Loss (Shijing), and Turbid White Discharge (Baizhuo)]

💡 术语：『梦遗』指睡眠中做梦伴遗精；『失精』指精液滑泄不固（包括梦遗、滑精等）；『白浊』指小便浑浊如米泔或尿道流出白色混浊分泌物。现代理解与对照：对应遗精、早泄/性功能障碍、尿道分泌物增多、前列腺液溢出、乳糜尿或泌尿生殖道感染等，需结合具体表现区分生理性偶发与病理性病变。

虚勞尿精灸第七椎兩旁各三十壮千或曲泉百壮

虚劳而小便夹精（尿精）：灸第七椎两旁各三十壮；《千金》说也可灸曲泉一百壮。

For spermatorrhea with urination in consumptive exhaustion, moxa on both sides of the 7th vertebra, 30 cones each. The Qianjin says one may alternatively moxa Ququan, 100 cones.

💡 『千』指《千金方》，『铜』指《铜人》，『明』指《明堂》，『甲』指《甲乙经》，这是本书引文出处的缩写。『壮』是艾炷的计数单位。第七椎旁约在膈俞BL17一带，旧法椎序与今略有出入。曲泉LR8。虚劳尿精相当于今天的遗精、精浊，多与肾虚不固有关。针灸系统考试中遗精常见的辨证是肾气不固、心肾不交、湿热下注。

虚勞白濁灸脾俞百壮或三焦俞腎俞章門各百壮

虚劳白浊：灸脾俞一百壮，或灸三焦俞、肾俞、章门各一百壮。

For turbid white discharge in consumptive exhaustion, moxa Pishu 100 cones, or Sanjiaoshu, Shenshu and Zhangmen 100 cones each.

💡 脾俞BL20、三焦俞BL22、肾俞BL23、章门LR13（脾之募穴）。白浊指小便混浊如米泔或尿道流出白色浊物，今天可见于慢性前列腺炎、尿道炎、乳糜尿等，古人多从脾虚湿浊或肾虚来理解。一百壮是古代直接灸的大剂量，今天临床多用温和灸或隔物灸，且先要明确诊断。

夢失精小便濁難灸腎俞百壮

梦中失精、小便浑浊不爽：灸肾俞一百壮。

For nocturnal emission with turbid, difficult urination, moxa Shenshu, 100 cones.

💡 肾俞BL23是肾之背俞穴，补肾固精的主穴。梦遗与精神、睡眠、前列腺及泌尿道炎症都可能相关，需要先排除器质性问题。

夢泄精灸中封五十

梦中泄精：灸中封五十壮。

For emission during dreams, moxa Zhongfeng, 50 cones.

💡 中封LR4，肝经经穴。肝经绕阴器，所以前阴、精关病常取肝经穴。

男子夢與人交精泄灸三隂穴五十

男子梦中与人交合而泄精：灸三阴交五十壮。

For men who emit semen while dreaming of intercourse, moxa Sanyinjiao, 50 cones.

💡 三阴穴即三阴交SP6，足三阴经交会穴，兼调肝、脾、肾。『梦与人交』属古人所说相火妄动、心肾不交。今天的看法是青年男性偶发梦遗属生理现象，只有频繁且伴明显不适时才算病理。

失精隂縮灸中封五十

失精并见阴茎内缩：灸中封五十壮。

For seminal loss with retraction of the genitals, moxa Zhongfeng, 50 cones.

💡 中封LR4。阴缩在古书里是厥阴经受寒或肝肾阳虚的表现，今天可见于严重受寒、重症或疼痛反应，不能只当作遗精的伴随症状。

隂痛溺血精出灸列缺俞五十

阴部疼痛、尿血、精液外泄：灸『列缺俞』五十壮。

For genital pain, bloody urine and leakage of semen, moxa 'Liequeshu', 50 cones.

💡 『列缺俞』不是WHO标准穴名，可能是传抄之误或古名，历代注家未必一致，此处存疑不硬配代码。列缺LU7属肺经络穴，通任脉，与泌尿生殖症状的联系是通过任脉。学习时可对照《千金》《外台》同条核对。尿血伴疼痛今天要排查结石、感染、肿瘤。

失精五藏虚竭灸曲骨端五十

失精、五脏虚竭：灸曲骨端五十壮。

For seminal loss with depletion of the five zang organs, moxa the tip of Qugu, 50 cones.

💡 曲骨CV2，位于耻骨联合上缘，任脉与足厥阴交会。『曲骨端』即曲骨穴一带。五脏虚竭是古代对严重虚损的描述，今天相当于慢性消耗状态，应查明原因。

失精隂縮莖痛灸大赫三十

失精、阴缩、阴茎疼痛：灸大赫三十壮。

For seminal loss, genital retraction and penile pain, moxa Dahe, 30 cones.

💡 大赫KI12，足少阴与冲脉交会。补肾固精常用穴，主治遗精、阳痿、阴部痛。

失精膝脛痛冷灸曲泉百壮

失精并见膝与小腿疼痛发冷：灸曲泉一百壮。

For seminal loss with cold pain in the knees and shins, moxa Ququan, 100 cones.

💡 曲泉LR8，肝经合穴，肝经循膝内侧上行，所以膝部症状与肝经有关。

腰脊冷疼溺濁灸脾募百壮並千

腰脊冷痛、小便浑浊：灸脾之募穴一百壮，此条同见于《千金》。

For cold pain of the lumbar spine with turbid urine, moxa the Spleen Front-Mu point, 100 cones; the same is found in the Qianjin.

💡 脾募即章门LR13（脾之募穴）。腰脊冷痛配尿浊，古人归于脾肾阳虚、湿浊下注。今天可见于慢性腰痛合并泌尿系感染或前列腺问题，要分开评估。

白濁漏精灸大椎骨尾1841骨並中間共三穴以繩量大椎至尾1841骨折中取中間穴别附

白浊、漏精：灸大椎骨和尾骶骨，再加两者中间一穴，共三穴；以绳量从大椎到尾骶骨的长度，对折后取中点定穴。另外附有说明。

For white turbidity and leaking semen, moxa the Dazhui vertebra, the coccygeal bone, and the midpoint between them, three points in all. Measure with a cord from Dazhui to the coccyx, fold it in half, and take the midpoint. An additional note is appended.

💡 原文中的『1841』是底本缺字，本书“背偻如1841”“1841胸”“1841尾”可证为『龟』字，“尾龟骨”即尾骨。大椎GV14，尾骶部约在长强GV1附近，中点约当脊柱中部，大致相当于命门GV4一带。以绳折量定穴是古代的『骨度折量』法，与今天的骨度分寸法同理。

太衝中封地機主精不足千

太冲、中封、地机主治精不足（见《千金》）。

Taichong, Zhongfeng and Diji govern insufficiency of essence (per the Qianjin).

💡 太冲LR3、中封LR4、地机SP8。『精不足』是阴精亏虚，今天看更接近性功能减退、精液质量下降等主诉。

中極蠡溝漏谷承扶至隂主小便不利失精明下同

中极、蠡沟、漏谷、承扶、至阴主治小便不利、失精（《明堂》下文同）。

Zhongji, Ligou, Lougu, Chengfu and Zhiyin govern difficult urination and seminal loss (the Mingtang, and likewise below).

💡 中极CV3（膀胱募穴）、蠡沟LR5、漏谷SP7、承扶BL36、至阴BL67。小便不利和失精并列，反映古人把前阴的排尿和藏精都归于肾、膀胱、肝经。

志室治失精

志室治疗失精。

Zhishi treats seminal loss.

💡 志室BL52，与肾俞平，主治遗精、阳痿、腰脊强痛。

小便淋3254

小便淋沥（标题；原文“淋3254”，3254为底本缺字，当为“沥”）。

Urinary dribbling and strangury (lin li; heading).

💡 『3254』是底本缺字，本书“淋3254”“遗3254”多处出现，据文意当为“沥”。淋沥指小便频数、涩痛、滴沥不尽，相当于今天泌尿系感染、结石、前列腺问题等引起的尿频、尿急、尿痛。

然谷主精溢大赫同

然谷主治精液外溢，大赫相同。

Rangu governs overflowing of semen; Dahe is the same.

💡 然谷KI2，足少阴荥穴。大赫KI12，见上。

䯒痠不能久立足一寒一熱

小腿酸痛不能久站，足部一侧寒、一侧热。

Aching of the shins so that one cannot stand for long, with one foot cold and the other hot.

💡 『䯒』即胫骨一带，与『胻』『骭』同类。此处为症状描述，原文承接上文，所属穴位应参考《千金》《明堂》。

行間治溺難白濁寒疝小腹腫

行间治疗排尿困难、白浊、寒疝、小腹肿胀。

Xingjian treats difficult urination, white turbidity, cold hernia and swelling of the lower abdomen.

💡 行间LR2，肝经荥穴，泻肝火。寒疝是寒邪凝滞引起的少腹拘急疼痛，睾丸牵引痛，今天可对应腹股沟疝、睾丸炎、附睾炎等。

腎俞治溺血便濁出精銅見勞瘵

肾俞治疗尿血、尿浊、遗精（《铜人》；另见劳瘵一篇）。

Shenshu treats bloody urine, turbid urine and emission (the Tongren; also see the chapter on consumption).

💡 肾俞BL23。劳瘵相当于今天的肺结核等慢性消耗性疾病，古代有时把虚劳和痨病并论。

膏肓俞治夢失精見勞

膏肓俞治疗梦遗（见劳瘵篇）。

Gaohuangshu treats nocturnal emission (see the chapter on consumption).

💡 膏肓BL43，主治虚劳、咳嗽、遗精。膏肓是古代所说药力难及之处，在这里作为补虚强壮穴。

至隂曲泉見風勞中極明下同治失精見淋

至阴、曲泉（见风劳篇）、中极（《明堂》下同）治疗失精（见淋篇）。

Zhiyin, Ququan (see the chapter on wind-consumption) and Zhongji (the Mingtang, likewise below) treat seminal loss (see the chapter on strangury).

💡 至阴BL67、曲泉LR8、中极CV3。这里是交叉引用，表示别处有更详细的论述。

志室治下腫失精夢泄精

志室治疗下部肿胀、失精、梦泄。

Zhishi treats swelling of the lower body, seminal loss and nocturnal emission.

💡 志室BL52。『下肿』可指阴囊或下肢肿，今天要区分肾源性、心源性、静脉性水肿。

灸三隂交二七壮夢斷神良千

灸三阴交十四壮，梦遗即止，效果很好（《千金》）。

Moxa Sanyinjiao for 14 cones; the dreams stop and the effect is excellent (the Qianjin).

💡 三阴交SP6。『二七壮』即十四壮。『神良』是古人对疗效的夸饰，不能当成临床保证。

虚勞尿精陽陵泉或隂陵泉隨年壮或十椎十九椎旁三十壮

虚劳尿精：灸阳陵泉或阴陵泉，壮数按年龄；或灸第十椎、第十九椎旁各三十壮。

For spermatorrhea in consumptive exhaustion, moxa Yanglingquan or Yinlingquan with the number of cones equal to the patient's age, or both sides of the 10th and 19th vertebrae, 30 cones each.

💡 阳陵泉GB34、阴陵泉SP9。『随年壮』指艾炷数等于年龄。古代椎序按二十一椎计，第十九椎约在骶部，十椎旁约在胆俞BL19一带，具体要核对原书。

耳聾腰痛失精食少膝以下清云云當灸京門五十壮十四椎百壮

耳聋、腰痛、失精、食少、膝以下发凉等，应灸京门五十壮，十四椎灸一百壮。

For deafness, lumbar pain, seminal loss, poor appetite and coldness below the knees, moxa Jingmen 50 cones and the 14th vertebra 100 cones.

💡 京门GB25，肾之募穴。十四椎约当命门GV4（第二腰椎下）。耳聋、腰痛、失精并见是肾虚的典型组合，这是『肾开窍于耳』『腰为肾之府』的临床应用。

五藏論曰心有三孔藏精汁三合千同則人之遺漏其因於心乎心動則遺漏從之欲免此患要養其心使不動可也

《五脏论》说：心有三个孔，藏精汁三合，与《千金》所说相同。那么人的遗精漏泄，莫非根源在心？心一动，遗漏就随之而来。想免除这个病患，关键是养心，使心不妄动。

The Wuzang Lun says the heart has three orifices storing three he of essence-fluid, the same as the Qianjin. So might seminal leakage originate in the heart? When the heart is stirred, leakage follows. To avoid this affliction, nourish the heart so that it does not move.

💡 这是中医『心肾不交』『君火动则相火随之』的理论：心藏神，神动则相火妄动，扰动精室。今天可解释为心理因素、性相关念头和精神压力会影响遗精频率，现代认知行为疗法也有类似处理。『心有三孔』是古代解剖想象，不是现代解剖事实。

其次則邪念或起必蚤抑之至㳺居士云不愁念起只恐覺遲是也服藥鍼灸斯為下矣然猶愈於不為也

其次，如果邪念偶然升起，一定要及早压住。游居士所说『不怕念头起，只怕觉察得迟』就是这个意思。吃药、针灸，已经是下策了，不过仍然胜过什么都不做。

Next, if improper thoughts arise, one must suppress them early. This is what Layman You meant by 'do not fear the rising of thoughts, only fear noticing them late.' Medicine, needling and moxibustion are the lowest recourse, yet still better than doing nothing.

💡 王执中在此引入禅宗式的『觉照』观念，把调心放在治疗之首，药针灸居次。这反映宋代医家『上工治神』的价值取向。今天可对照为：生活方式、心理调节是一线措施，药物和针灸辅助。

大便不通

（标题）大便不通。

(Heading) Obstructed defecation.

💡 相当于今天的便秘。古人分热秘、气秘、冷秘、虚秘等，针灸常取天枢ST25、大肠俞BL25、支沟TE6等。注意今天要排除肠梗阻、肿瘤等器质性原因。

大鍾中窌石門承山太衝中管太谿承筋主大便難千

大钟、中髎、石门、承山、太冲、中脘、太溪、承筋主治大便困难（《千金》）。

Dazhong, Zhongliao, Shimen, Chengshan, Taichong, Zhongwan, Taixi and Chengjin govern difficult defecation (the Qianjin).

💡 大钟KI4、中髎BL33、石门CV5、承山BL57、太冲LR3、中脘CV12、太溪KI3、承筋BL56。『中窌』即中髎，『中管』即中脘。承山、承筋是治痔和便难的常用小腿穴。

崑崙主不得大便

昆仑主治大便不得出。

Kunlun governs inability to defecate.

💡 昆仑BL60，膀胱经经穴。膀胱经行于背腰，与肛肠病的联系多通过经脉循行解释。

肓俞主大便乾腹中切痛

肓俞主治大便干燥、腹中剧痛。

Huangshu governs dry stool and sharp pain in the abdomen.

💡 肓俞KI16，脐旁0.5寸，足少阴与冲脉交会。

石關主大便閉塞氣結心堅滿

石关主治大便闭塞、气结、心下坚满。

Shiguan governs blockage of the stool, qi binding, and hard fullness below the heart.

💡 石关KI18，脐上3寸旁开0.5寸。『心坚满』指心下（胃脘）硬满，相当于腹胀、上腹压痛，要警惕肠梗阻。

承山見轉筋太谿見傷寒無汗治大便難銅大鍾銅見淋

承山（见转筋篇）、太溪（见伤寒无汗篇）治大便困难（《铜人》）；大钟（《铜人》，见淋篇）。

Chengshan (see the chapter on cramps) and Taixi (see the chapter on cold damage without sweating) treat difficult defecation (the Tongren); Dazhong (the Tongren, see the chapter on strangury).

💡 承山BL57、太溪KI3、大钟KI4。这类句子多是转引和互见，用来对照不同医书。

石關治大便祕澁肓俞治大便燥見腰痛

石关治疗大便秘涩，肓俞治疗大便燥结（见腰痛篇）。

Shiguan treats constipation with dryness; Huangshu treats dry stool (see the chapter on lumbar pain).

💡 石关KI18、肓俞KI16。『秘澁』指排便不畅、粪质干结。

中注治小腹有熱大便堅燥不利

中注治疗小腹有热、大便坚硬干燥不通利。

Zhongzhu treats heat in the lower abdomen with hard, dry and inhibited stool.

💡 中注KI15。这是热秘，与今天所说的肠道燥热、津液不足型便秘相应。

太白治腰痛大便難太衝治足寒大便難

太白治疗腰痛、大便困难；太冲治疗足寒、大便困难。

Taibai treats lumbar pain with difficult defecation; Taichong treats cold feet with difficult defecation.

💡 太白SP3，脾经输穴；太冲LR3，肝经输穴。脾主运化，肝主疏泄，所以便秘可从脾、肝论治。

石關膀胱俞療腹痛大便難明下

石关、膀胱俞治疗腹痛、大便困难（《明堂》下文）。

Shiguan and Pangguangshu treat abdominal pain and difficult defecation (the Mingtang, below).

💡 石关KI18、膀胱俞BL28。膀胱俞在骶部，与大肠俞一样常用于二便病。

大便難灸七椎旁各一寸七壮千又承筋三壮大便不通大敦四壮大便閉塞氣結心堅滿石門百壮餘見千金

大便困难：灸第七椎旁各一寸，七壮（《千金》）；又灸承筋三壮。大便不通：灸大敦四壮。大便闭塞、气结、心下坚满：灸石门一百壮。其余见《千金》。

For difficult defecation, moxa 1 cun lateral to the 7th vertebra, 7 cones (the Qianjin), and also Chengjin 3 cones. For obstructed stool, moxa Dadun 4 cones. For blocked stool with qi binding and hard fullness below the heart, moxa Shimen 100 cones. The rest is in the Qianjin.

💡 承筋BL56、大敦LR1、石门CV5。第七椎旁的位置今天按膈俞BL17附近理解，但原文取旁开一寸，和BL17的一寸五分略有差别。注意：石门在古代针灸禁忌里有『女子禁针灸，恐绝孕』之说，学习时留意，今天临床对此多持谨慎态度。

腹中有積大便祕巴豆肉為餅置臍中灸三壮即通神效耆域宻兊治大便祕詳見既効

腹中有积、大便秘结：把巴豆肉做成饼，放在脐中，灸三壮，立刻就通，效果神奇。耆域的秘方治大便秘，详见《既效方》。

For abdominal accumulation with constipation, make a cake of croton seed kernel, place it on the navel and moxa it 3 cones; the bowels open at once with marvelous effect. Qi Yu's secret formula for constipation is detailed in the Jixiao fang.

💡 这是隔巴豆饼灸神阙CV8。巴豆是峻下药，有强烈刺激性和毒性，外用也可能致皮肤发泡溃烂，今天不建议自行操作。此条只作为古代隔物灸的例子来学习。『耆域』是传说中的西域医者，宋代医书常引他的方。

大小便不通

（标题）大小便不通。

(Heading) Obstruction of both urination and defecation.

💡 古称『关格』『癃闭』并见者，今天需警惕肠梗阻、尿潴留、严重脱水或肾功能问题，应立即就医。

豐隆主大小便澁難明同長强明下同

丰隆主治大小便涩滞困难（《明堂》同）；长强（《明堂》下同）。

Fenglong governs difficult and sluggish urination and defecation (the Mingtang agrees); Changqiang (the Mingtang, likewise below).

💡 丰隆ST40、长强GV1。长强位于尾骨尖端与肛门之间，主治痔、便秘、脱肛。

小腸俞主大小便難淋癃

小肠俞主治大小便困难、淋证、癃闭。

Xiaochangshu governs difficult urination and defecation, strangury and retention.

💡 小肠俞BL27，平第一骶后孔。『癃』指小便不通，『淋』指小便淋沥涩痛。

包肓主癃閉下重大小便難

胞肓主治癃闭、下腹重坠、大小便困难。

Baohuang governs urinary retention, heaviness in the lower body, and difficult urination and defecation.

💡 胞肓BL53，平第二骶后孔，旁开3寸。原文『包肓』即胞肓。

水道主三焦約大小便不通又云主婦人營衝四穴主大小便不利

水道主治三焦约结、大小便不通；又说主治妇人营冲四穴，主治大小便不利。

Shuidao governs constriction of the Triple Burner with blocked urination and defecation; it is also said that the four points of the Ying-Chong region in women govern inhibited urination and defecation.

💡 水道ST28，位于脐下3寸旁开2寸，主治小腹胀满、小便不利。『三焦约』是三焦气化失常，水道不利。『营冲四穴』具体所指不详，可能是下腹部冲脉相关穴位。

太谿主大便難尿黄中注浮郄主小腹熱大便堅

（原文为『太溪主大便难尿黄中注浮郄主小腹热大便坚』）太溪主治大便困难、小便黄；中注、浮郄主治小腹有热、大便坚硬。

Taixi governs difficult defecation and yellow urine; Zhongzhu and Fuxi govern heat in the lower abdomen with hard stool.

💡 太溪KI3、中注KI15、浮郄BL38。此条在原文中跨行，断句时已把行尾『大便』与下一行『坚』接起来。

白環俞見腰脊扶承見痔大腸俞治大小便不利銅見腹脹會隂治不得大小便見隂痛千同

白环俞（见腰脊篇）、扶承（见痔篇）、大肠俞治疗大小便不利（《铜人》，见腹胀篇）；会阴治疗大小便不得（见阴痛篇，《千金》同）。

Baihuanshu (see the chapter on lumbar spine), Fucheng (see the chapter on hemorrhoids) and Dachangshu treat inhibited urination and defecation (the Tongren, see the chapter on abdominal distension); Huiyin treats inability to urinate or defecate (see the chapter on genital pain, the Qianjin agrees).

💡 白环俞BL30、扶承即承扶BL36、大肠俞BL25、会阴CV1。会阴是任脉起点，针刺要谨慎避开尿道和直肠。

浮郄治小腸熱大腸結見筋急膀胱俞療大小便難尿赤明交信療大小便難

浮郄治疗小肠有热、大肠郁结（见筋急篇）；膀胱俞治疗大小便困难、小便赤（《明堂》）；交信治疗大小便困难。

Fuxi treats heat in the small intestine and binding in the large intestine (see the chapter on sinew tension); Pangguangshu treats difficult urination and defecation with red urine (the Mingtang); Jiaoxin treats difficult urination and defecation.

💡 浮郄BL38、膀胱俞BL28、交信KI8（阴跷脉郄穴）。

一卒傷寒大小便不通予與五苓散而皆通五苓固利小便矣而大便亦通者津液生故也或小便通而大便尚不通宜用2869九道之必用方婦人老人大便祕用麻子蘇子煮粥食最佳

有一个人突然得了伤寒，大小便都不通，我给他服五苓散，两种都通了。五苓散本来是利小便的，大便也通，是因为津液生了。如果小便通了而大便还没通，应当用（缺字）九道之必用方。妇人和老人的便秘，用麻子、紫苏子煮粥吃最好。

A man suddenly struck by cold damage had both urination and defecation blocked. I gave Wuling San and both opened. Wuling San indeed promotes urination, but the stool also opened because fluids were generated. If urination opens but the stool still does not, one should use the (lost-glyph) Jiudao Biyong Fang. For constipation in women and the elderly, congee cooked with hemp seed and perilla seed is best.

💡 『2869』是底本缺字，据上下文是某个方名的一部分，无法确认。五苓散（茯苓、猪苓、泽泻、白术、桂枝）化气行水，小便利则津液得以布散，所以大便也可通，这是『利小便以实大便』反向的应用。麻子仁、紫苏子富含油脂，润肠通便，对老人、产后津亏便秘有效，今天仍常用。患者若为急性发热合并二便皆闭，应及时就医。

小便不禁遺尿附

（标题）小便不禁、遗尿（附）。

(Heading) Urinary incontinence and enuresis (appended).

💡 小便不禁多与肾气不固、脾肺气虚、下焦虚寒有关。今天对应尿失禁、遗尿症、神经源性膀胱等，成人突发尿失禁要排查神经系统病变。

承漿主小便不禁見便黄關元又主婦人小便數泄不止

承浆主治小便不禁（见便黄篇）；关元又主治妇人小便频数、泄出不止。

Chengjiang governs urinary incontinence (see the chapter on yellow stool); Guanyuan also governs frequent, unstoppable urination in women.

💡 承浆CV24，关元CV4（小肠募穴）。关元补肾培元，是固摄类病证的要穴。

涌泉主小便數少府主隂暴痛遺尿千

涌泉主治小便频数；少府主治阴部突然疼痛、遗尿（《千金》）。

Yongquan governs frequent urination; Shaofu governs sudden genital pain and enuresis (the Qianjin).

💡 涌泉KI1、少府HT8。少府是心经荥穴，心火下移小肠、膀胱，所以与小便病相关。

關門中府甲作委中神門主遺尿

关门、中府（《甲乙经》作委中）、神门主治遗尿。

Guanmen, Zhongfu (the Jiayijing reads Weizhong) and Shenmen govern enuresis.

💡 关门ST22、中府LU1、委中BL40、神门HT7。这里体现版本异文：『中府』在《甲乙经》作『委中』，学习时要知道古书可能抄错，需对校。

隂陵泉陽陵泉主失禁遺尿不自知

阴陵泉、阳陵泉主治失禁、遗尿而自己不知道。

Yinlingquan and Yanglingquan govern incontinence and enuresis without awareness.

💡 阴陵泉SP9、阳陵泉GB34。『不自知』提示意识障碍或神经病变的可能，今天要评估脑、脊髓问题。

太衝主女遺尿見疝關門治遺溺善滿銅

太冲主治女子遗尿（见疝篇）；关门治疗遗尿、腹胀易满（《铜人》）。

Taichong governs enuresis in women (see the chapter on hernia); Guanmen treats enuresis and a tendency to fullness (the Tongren).

💡 太冲LR3、关门ST22。

箕門見淋通里見傷寒千同大敦見疝膀胱俞見便赤太衝見小便不利委中見腰脊

箕门（见淋篇）、通里（见伤寒篇，《千金》同）、大敦（见疝篇）、膀胱俞（见便赤篇）、太冲（见小便不利篇）、委中（见腰脊篇）。

Jimen (see the chapter on strangury), Tongli (see the chapter on cold damage; the Qianjin agrees), Dadun (see the chapter on hernia), Pangguangshu (see the chapter on red urine), Taichong (see the chapter on inhibited urination), Weizhong (see the chapter on lumbar spine).

💡 箕门SP11、通里HT5、大敦LR1、膀胱俞BL28、太冲LR3、委中BL40。这是交叉索引性的穴位列表。

神門治遺溺見心煩隂包治遺溺不禁見腰痛

神门治疗遗尿（见心烦篇）；阴包治疗遗尿不禁（见腰痛篇）。

Shenmen treats enuresis (see the chapter on vexation); Yinbao treats incontinent enuresis (see the chapter on lumbar pain).

💡 神门HT7、阴包LR9。

遺溺灸陽陵泉或足陽明各隨年千

遗尿：灸阳陵泉，或足阳明经的穴位，壮数各按年龄（《千金》）。

For enuresis, moxa Yanglingquan or points of the Foot Yangming, each with cones equal to the patient's age (the Qianjin).

💡 阳陵泉GB34。足阳明具体穴位没有写明，可能指足三里ST36等。

遺溺失禁出不自知灸隂陵泉隨年

遗尿失禁、排出而不自知：灸阴陵泉，壮数按年龄。

For incontinence with unconscious discharge, moxa Yinlingquan with cones equal to the patient's age.

💡 阴陵泉SP9，脾经合穴，健脾利湿，兼可固摄。

小便失禁灸大敦或行間七壮

小便失禁：灸大敦或行间，七壮。

For urinary incontinence, moxa Dadun or Xingjian, 7 cones.

💡 大敦LR1、行间LR2，肝经井、荥穴。肝经环阴器，所以前阴病常取肝经。

尿床灸臍下横文七壮

尿床：灸脐下的横纹处，七壮。

For bedwetting, moxa the transverse crease below the navel, 7 cones.

💡 『脐下横文』所指位置不够明确，可能是下腹部皮肤横纹处，不是标准WHO穴名，不硬配代码。遗尿症在今天要考虑睡眠深度、膀胱容量、抗利尿激素、心理因素，儿童遗尿首选行为训练和夜间警报器等。

婦人遺尿灸横骨七壮

妇人遗尿：灸横骨七壮。

For enuresis in women, moxa Henggu, 7 cones.

💡 横骨KI11，在耻骨联合上缘旁开0.5寸，足少阴与冲脉交会。

小兒遺尿灸臍下寸半隨年又灸大敦三壮餘見千金

小儿遗尿：灸脐下一寸半，壮数按年龄；又灸大敦三壮。其余见《千金》。

For enuresis in children, moxa 1.5 cun below the navel with cones equal to the child's age, and also Dadun 3 cones. The rest is in the Qianjin.

💡 脐下1.5寸是气海CV6。大敦LR1。儿童施灸壮数应大幅减少，今天多用温和灸或艾条熏灸，不直接化脓灸，也不能照搬『随年壮』。

曲泉隂谷隂陵泉復溜此諸穴斷小便利大佳不損陽氣亦云止遺尿

曲泉、阴谷、阴陵泉、复溜，这几个穴位用来截止小便过多（小便利）特别好，不损伤阳气，也有说法能止遗尿。

Ququan, Yingu, Yinlingquan and Fuliu are excellent for cutting off excessive urination without harming yang qi, and are also said to stop enuresis.

💡 曲泉LR8、阴谷KI10、阴陵泉SP9、复溜KI7，皆是肝、脾、肾经的合穴或经穴。『不损阳气』强调这些穴位是通过调节而不是强行收涩。

大便不禁餘見泄瀉

（标题）大便失禁（其余见泄泻篇）。

(Heading) Fecal incontinence (the rest is under diarrhea).

💡 大便不禁主要见于脾肾阳虚、中气下陷。今天对应肛门括约肌功能障碍、神经病变、慢性腹泻、产后损伤等。

大腸俞次窌主大小便利

大肠俞、次髎主治大小便失调。

Dachangshu and Ciliao govern disorders of urination and defecation.

💡 大肠俞BL25、次髎BL32（第二骶后孔）。次髎常用于盆底、泌尿生殖症状。

陽綱主大便不節明同腸鳴泄注小便赤黄

阳纲主治大便失节、肠鸣泄泻、小便赤黄（《明堂》同）。

Yanggang governs unregulated defecation, borborygmus, diarrhea and dark yellow urine (the Mingtang agrees).

💡 阳纲BL48，平第十胸椎旁开3寸，属膀胱经第二侧线，对应胆。

承扶主尻中腫大便直出隂胞有寒小便不利

承扶主治臀部肿胀、大便直接排出、阴部胞宫有寒、小便不利。

Chengfu governs swelling in the buttock, stool passing straight out, cold in the genital and uterine region, and inhibited urination.

💡 承扶BL36，位于臀横纹中点。『阴胞』指前阴及胞宫一带。

屈骨端主大便泄數小便不利并灸天樞

屈骨端主治大便泄泻次数多、小便不利，并灸天枢。

Qugu duan governs frequent loose stools and inhibited urination; also moxa Tianshu.

💡 屈骨端即曲骨CV2。天枢ST25是大肠募穴，调理肠腑最常用。

丹田主泄利不禁小便絞痛關元療洩痢虛脹小便難明

丹田主治泄泻不禁、小便绞痛；关元治疗泄痢、虚胀、小便困难（《明堂》）。

Dantian governs uncontrolled diarrhea and cramping urinary pain; Guanyuan treats diarrhea, deficient distension and difficult urination (the Mingtang).

💡 丹田在这里通常指石门CV5（《甲乙经》别名），也有地方指关元CV4。关元CV4。学习时要注意『丹田』在古书中可指不同位置，不同章节要对照。

䰟門治大便不節銅

魂门治疗大便失节（《铜人》）。

Hunmen treats unregulated defecation (the Tongren).

💡 魂门BL47，平第九胸椎旁开3寸，对应肝。

老小大便失禁灸兩足大指去甲一寸三壮又灸大指岐間各三壮千

老人、小儿大便失禁：灸两足大趾距趾甲一寸处，三壮；又灸大趾趾缝间各三壮（《千金》）。

For fecal incontinence in the elderly and children, moxa a point 1 cun from the nail of the big toe on both feet, 3 cones, and also between the big toe and the next toe, 3 cones each (the Qianjin).

💡 『去甲一寸』约当大敦LR1附近稍上，『岐间』约在行间LR2一带，原文并未给标准穴名。老人小儿用灸要控制壮数和温度，避免烫伤。

三里主霍亂遺失

足三里主治霍乱、大小便遗失。

Sanli governs cholera-like disorder with loss of control of the bowels.

💡 足三里ST36。古代『霍乱』是上吐下泻，不等同于现代由霍乱弧菌引起的霍乱，但包括了急性胃肠炎等。今天出现严重脱水、米泔样大便要立即就医补液。

大便不禁病亦惙矣神闕石門丹田屈骨端等皆是穴處宜速灸之予頃患脾泄醫謂有積以冷藥利之大便不禁服鎮靈丹十餘丸午夜各數丸而愈今人服此丹三五丸不效則不服是以一勺水救輿薪火也可乎哉

大便失禁的病，也已经很危弱了。神阙、石门、丹田、曲骨等都是该灸的穴位，应当赶快灸。我前些时候患脾泄，医生说有积滞，用寒凉药泻下，结果大便失禁。我服镇灵丹十几丸，午时和夜里各几丸，就好了。现在的人吃这个药三五丸没效就不吃了，这是用一勺水去救一车柴火的火，怎么可以呢？

Fecal incontinence indicates a person who is already weakened. Shenque, Shimen, Dantian and Qugu are the points to moxa quickly. Some time ago I suffered spleen diarrhea; a physician said there was accumulation and purged me with cold medicine, after which I became incontinent. I took over ten Zhenling pills, several at noon and several at night, and recovered. People now take three or five pills, see no effect and stop. That is like fighting a burning cartload of firewood with a ladle of water; how can it work?

💡 神阙CV8、石门CV5、丹田（见上）、曲骨CV2。王执中本人的经验：寒凉泻药使脾阳受损反致滑脱，服温热的镇灵丹而愈。『镇灵丹』方药成分此处未写，不要自行配服。这段说明古代医家重视『病重药轻』的问题，今天应在医生指导下评估病因，不能把个案当成通则。

泄瀉餘見吐瀉

（标题）泄泻（其余见吐泻篇）。

(Heading) Diarrhea (the rest is under vomiting and diarrhea).

💡 泄泻在中医有寒湿、湿热、伤食、肝郁、脾虚、肾虚等分型。今天要区分急性感染性腹泻、慢性腹泻（肠易激综合征、炎症性肠病、乳糜泻）等。

曲泉治泄利四支不舉銅見疝

曲泉治疗泄泻、四肢无力抬不起来（《铜人》，见疝篇）。

Ququan treats diarrhea with inability to lift the limbs (the Tongren, see the chapter on hernia).

💡 曲泉LR8。四肢不举是脾虚或电解质紊乱的表现，今天要想到低钾。

腹結治腹寒泄利見臍痛

腹结治疗腹寒、泄泻（见脐痛篇）。

Fujie treats abdominal cold with diarrhea (see the chapter on umbilical pain).

💡 腹结SP14，位于大横下1.3寸。

神闕治泄利不止小兒妳利不絶腹大繞臍痛

神阙治疗泄泻不止、小儿乳痢不断、腹大、绕脐疼痛。

Shenque treats uncontrolled diarrhea, ceaseless milk-related diarrhea in infants, enlarged abdomen and pain around the navel.

💡 神阙CV8即肚脐，古代禁针，宜隔盐灸、隔姜灸。小儿『妳利』即乳食不化的腹泻，今天婴儿腹泻要重视脱水，不要只靠灸。

氣穴治婦人泄利不止見月事

气穴治疗妇人泄泻不止（见月事篇）。

Qixue treats uncontrolled diarrhea in women (see the chapter on menstruation).

💡 气穴KI13，在脐下3寸旁开0.5寸，足少阴与冲脉交会。

陽綱治大便泄利意舎治大便滑泄並見腹脹

阳纲治疗大便泄泻，意舍治疗大便滑泄，都见腹胀篇。

Yanggang treats diarrhea; Yishe treats slippery diarrhea; both are under abdominal distension.

💡 阳纲BL48、意舍BL49（平第十一胸椎旁开3寸，对应脾）。

梁門治大腸滑泄榖不化見積氣關門治泄利不欲食見積氣

梁门治疗大肠滑泄、谷物不化（见积气篇）；关门治疗泄泻、不想吃东西（见积气篇）。

Liangmen treats slippery diarrhea of the large intestine with undigested grain (see the chapter on accumulated qi); Guanmen treats diarrhea with loss of appetite (see the same chapter).

💡 梁门ST21、关门ST22。『谷不化』是完谷不化，脾胃虚寒的典型症状。

天樞治泄利食不化三焦俞治水榖不化欲泄注見腹脹

天枢治疗泄泻、食物不化；三焦俞治疗水谷不化、想泄泻（见腹胀篇）。

Tianshu treats diarrhea with undigested food; Sanjiaoshu treats undigested food and water with urge to purge (see the chapter on abdominal distension).

💡 天枢ST25、三焦俞BL22。

懸樞治水榖不化下利見積聚脊中治温病積聚下利

悬枢治疗水谷不化、泄泻（见积聚篇）；脊中治疗温病、积聚、泄泻。

Xuanshu treats undigested food and water with diarrhea (see the chapter on accumulations); Jizhong treats warm disease, accumulations and diarrhea.

💡 悬枢GV5、脊中GV6，督脉穴。督脉与脊柱相关，对肠道症状可作为辅助。

中髎治腹脹下利食泄脾俞治泄利見腹脹膀胱俞治泄利腹痛

中髎治疗腹胀、泄泻、食泄；脾俞治疗泄泻（见腹胀篇）；膀胱俞治疗泄泻、腹痛。

Zhongliao treats abdominal distension, diarrhea and food-diarrhea; Pishu treats diarrhea (see the chapter on abdominal distension); Pangguangshu treats diarrhea with abdominal pain.

💡 中髎BL33、脾俞BL20、膀胱俞BL28。

大腸俞腎俞治洞洩食不化見勞瘵會陽治腹中冷氣洩利不止

大肠俞、肾俞治疗洞泄、食物不化（见劳瘵篇）；会阳治疗腹中冷气、泄泻不止。

Dachangshu and Shenshu treat dong-diarrhea with undigested food (see the chapter on consumption); Huiyang treats cold qi in the abdomen with uncontrolled diarrhea.

💡 大肠俞BL25、肾俞BL23、会阳BL35。『洞泄』是泄下急迫如倾泻，多属脾肾阳虚或寒湿。

京門治小腹急腫腸鳴洞洩髎樞引痛三間治腹滿腸鳴洞洩

京门治疗小腹拘急肿胀、肠鸣、洞泄、髎枢牵引疼痛；三间治疗腹满、肠鸣、洞泄。

Jingmen treats tension and swelling of the lower abdomen, borborygmus, dong-diarrhea and pain referring to the hip joint; Sanjian treats abdominal fullness, borborygmus and dong-diarrhea.

💡 京门GB25、三间LI3（大肠经输穴）。『髎枢』指髋部环跳GB30一带。

然谷治兒洞洩見口噤關元療腹洩不止明下見賁豚

然谷治疗小儿洞泄（见口噤篇）；关元治疗腹泻不止（《明堂》下，见奔豚篇）。

Rangu treats dong-diarrhea in children (see the chapter on lockjaw); Guanyuan treats uncontrolled diarrhea (the Mingtang, below; see the chapter on running piglet).

💡 然谷KI2、关元CV4。

京門然谷隂陵泉主洞泄不化千腎俞章門主寒中洞泄不化

京门、然谷、阴陵泉主治洞泄、食物不化（《千金》）；肾俞、章门主治中寒、洞泄、食物不化。

Jingmen, Rangu and Yinlingquan govern dong-diarrhea with undigested food (the Qianjin); Shenshu and Zhangmen govern cold in the middle with dong-diarrhea and undigested food.

💡 京门GB25、然谷KI2、阴陵泉SP9、肾俞BL23、章门LR13。这里把肾、脾、肝的穴位合用，对应脾肾阳虚型泄泻。

京門崑崙主洞泄體痛長强主頭重洞泄明下云洞洩不禁

京门、昆仑主治洞泄、身体疼痛；长强主治头重、洞泄（《明堂》下文说是洞泄不禁）。

Jingmen and Kunlun govern dong-diarrhea with body pain; Changqiang governs heaviness of the head and dong-diarrhea (the Mingtang says below: uncontrolled dong-diarrhea).

💡 京门GB25、昆仑BL60、长强GV1。此条原文跨行，已把『洞』与『泄体痛』接起来。

隂陵泉隱白主胷中熱暴泄大腸俞主腸鳴腹䐜腫暴洩

阴陵泉、隐白主治胸中热、突然泄泻；大肠俞主治肠鸣、腹胀肿、突然泄泻。

Yinlingquan and Yinbai govern heat in the chest and sudden diarrhea; Dachangshu governs borborygmus, abdominal swelling and sudden diarrhea.

💡 阴陵泉SP9、隐白SP1、大肠俞BL25。『暴泄』指急性腹泻，今天首先要防脱水。

三焦俞小腸俞下窌意舎章門主腸鳴腹脹欲泄注會陽主腹中有寒泄注腸澼便血束骨主腸澼泄

三焦俞、小肠俞、下髎、意舍、章门主治肠鸣、腹胀、欲泄；会阳主治腹中有寒、泄泻、肠澼便血；束骨主治肠澼、泄泻。

Sanjiaoshu, Xiaochangshu, Xialiao, Yishe and Zhangmen govern borborygmus, abdominal distension and urge to purge; Huiyang governs abdominal cold, diarrhea, intestinal bi and bloody stool; Shugu governs intestinal bi and diarrhea.

💡 三焦俞BL22、小肠俞BL27、下髎BL34、意舍BL49、章门LR13、会阳BL35、束骨BL65。『肠澼』是古称痢疾或便脓血一类病，见后文。

天樞主冬月重感於寒則泄當臍痛腸胃間遊氣切痛若心腹痛而後泄此寒氣客於腸間云云灸關元百壮服當歸縮砂湯指泄瀉宜先灸臍中次灸關元等穴

天枢主治：冬天再次感受寒邪就泄泻，脐周疼痛，肠胃之间有游走的气，疼痛如切。如果先有心腹痛而后泄，这是寒气停留在肠间……灸关元一百壮，并服当归缩砂汤（出《指迷》）。泄泻应该先灸脐中，再灸关元等穴。

Tianshu governs: when one is repeatedly struck by cold in winter, diarrhea results, with pain around the navel and sharp pain from wandering qi between the stomach and intestines. If heart-abdominal pain precedes the diarrhea, cold qi is lodged in the intestines... Moxa Guanyuan 100 cones and take Danggui Suosha Tang (from the Zhimi). For diarrhea one should first moxa the navel, then Guanyuan and other points.

💡 天枢ST25、关元CV4、神阙CV8。当归缩砂汤方药此处不详。『冬月重感于寒则泄』的思路是寒邪伤脾胃阳气，用灸温中散寒。注意：慢性腹泻要先明确是否感染、炎症、肿瘤，灸疗只是辅助。

飱泄

（标题）飧泄。

(Heading) Sun-diarrhea (undigested-food diarrhea).

💡 飧泄指泻下完谷不化，多因脾胃阳虚或风邪内陷，今天相当于消化不良性腹泻或脾虚泄泻。

中窌主腹脹飱泄

中髎主治腹胀、飧泄。

Zhongliao governs abdominal distension and sun-diarrhea.

💡 中髎BL33。

下亷治小腹痛飱洩次指間痛脣乾涎出不覺不得汗出毛髪焦脱肉少氣胃中熱不嗜食

下廉治疗小腹痛、飧泄，次指间疼痛，嘴唇干燥、口涎自出而不觉，不得出汗，毛发枯焦脱落，肌肉消瘦、气少，胃中有热，不想吃东西。

Xialian treats lower abdominal pain, sun-diarrhea, pain between the toes, dry lips with unnoticed drooling, absence of sweating, scorched and falling hair, wasting and shortness of breath, heat in the stomach and no desire to eat.

💡 这里的下廉指足阳明经的下巨虚ST39（《灵枢》称下廉）。上廉指上巨虚ST37。注意不要与手阳明大肠经的下廉LI8混淆。症状繁多，反映胃肠病影响全身营养。

上亷見脇痛治飱洩隂陵泉主婦人飱泄見疝瘕

上廉（见胁痛篇）治疗飧泄；阴陵泉主治妇人飧泄（见疝瘕篇）。

Shanglian (see the chapter on hypochondriac pain) treats sun-diarrhea; Yinlingquan governs sun-diarrhea in women (see the chapter on hernia and conglomerations).

💡 上廉即上巨虚ST37，大肠下合穴，是治肠病的重要穴位；阴陵泉SP9。

素問言春傷於風夏必飱泄茍知傷於風而得之則藥自可治雖不著艾未為害也

《素问》说：春天伤于风，夏天必然发生飧泄。只要知道是由伤风而得，就用药自然可以治好，即使不灸艾也没有妨害。

The Suwen says: injury by wind in spring will certainly cause sun-diarrhea in summer. If one knows it arose from wind, medicines can cure it, and not applying moxa does no harm.

💡 出自《素问·阴阳应象大论》『春伤于风，夏生飧泄』。这是『四时五脏阴阳』的发病规律：春季肝木旺，风邪伤脾，到夏季湿热外加，脾运失调。王执中坦率承认并非所有泄泻都必须灸，说明他重视辨证。

本事方云飱泄者食榖不化也春時木旺肝生風邪淫於脾經至夏引冷當風故多飱泄宜芎藭丸芎藭神麯白术附子等分細末糊圓梧子大每服三五十丸米飲下治脾濕而泄者萬無不中其用芎除濕有理故載于此

《本事方》说：飧泄就是吃下的谷物不消化。春天木旺，肝产生风邪，侵入脾经，到夏天又贪凉、当风，所以多发飧泄，宜用芎䓖丸：川芎、神曲、白术、附子等分，研细末，用糊做成梧桐子大的丸，每次三五十丸，米汤送下。治疗脾湿而泄的，万无不中。其用川芎除湿有道理，所以载在这里。

The Bencao Shi (Ben Shi Fang) says: sun-diarrhea is diarrhea with undigested grain. In spring the wood is strong and the liver generates wind evil, which invades the spleen channel; by summer, indulgence in cold and exposure to wind cause frequent sun-diarrhea. Chuanxiong Wan is appropriate: chuanxiong, shenqu, baizhu and fuzi in equal parts, ground fine and made into pills with paste, the size of parasol seeds; take thirty to fifty pills each time with rice water. For spleen-damp diarrhea it never fails. Its use of chuanxiong to remove dampness is reasonable, so it is recorded here.

💡 《本事方》（《普济本事方》）是南宋许叔微所著。药物：川芎、神曲、白术、附子。附子有毒，需炮制，用量须在医师指导下。『万无不中』是宋人对疗效的夸饰。方理：风木乘脾，川芎行气祛风，白术健脾除湿，附子温阳，神曲消食。

溏泄

（标题）溏泄。

(Heading) Loose stool diarrhea.

💡 溏泄指大便稀软不成形，多由脾虚湿盛引起，今天对应功能性腹泻、慢性肠炎等。

三隂交治溏洩食不化銅見腹脹地機見水腫治溏洩

三阴交治疗溏泄、食物不化（《铜人》，见腹胀篇）；地机（见水肿篇）治疗溏泄。

Sanyinjiao treats loose diarrhea with undigested food (the Tongren, see the chapter on abdominal distension); Diji (see the chapter on edema) treats loose diarrhea.

💡 三阴交SP6、地机SP8（脾经郄穴）。

地機主溏瘕腹痛藏痹千太衝等主溏泄見痢

地机主治溏瘕、腹痛、藏痹（《千金》）；太冲等主治溏泄（见痢篇）。

Diji governs loose conglomeration, abdominal pain and zang-bi (the Qianjin); Taichong and others govern loose diarrhea (see the chapter on dysentery).

💡 地机SP8、太冲LR3。『溏瘕』是下利而腹内有积块之类。『藏痹』为内脏痹阻，具体含义依各家不同。

予嘗患痹疼既愈而溏利者久之因灸臍中遂不登溷連三日灸之三夕不登溷若灸溏泄臍中第一三隂交等穴乃其次也

我曾得过痹痛，痹痛好了之后却长期溏泻，因此灸脐中，竟然不再去厕所，连灸三天，三个晚上都不需上厕所。如果灸溏泄，脐中是第一，三阴交等穴是其次。

I once suffered painful bi; after it healed I had prolonged loose stools. I moxaed the navel and no longer needed the toilet, even for three days of moxa and three nights. For loose diarrhea, the navel is first, and Sanyinjiao and similar points are second.

💡 神阙CV8、三阴交SP6。这是作者的个案经验。隔盐灸或隔姜灸神阙，对虚寒性泄泻确有温中止泻作用。个案不能直接推广，有血便、发热、体重下降者要先就医。

本事方云一親每五更初必溏痢一次者數月有人云此名腎泄腎感隂氣而然服五味子散愈五味子二兩呉茱萸半兩細粒緑色者並炒香熟為末每服二錢陳米飲下其論溏利有理故附載之予舊患溏利每天曉必如厠人敎贖豆附丸服即愈其方不可得也他年再患此只用薑煎附子加豆蔲服愈

《本事方》说：有一位亲戚每天五更之初一定要腹泻一次，持续几个月。有人说这叫肾泄，肾感受阴气所致，服五味子散而好。五味子二两，吴茱萸半两（取细粒绿色的），都炒香炒熟，研为末，每次二钱，用陈米汤送下。它对溏泻的论述有道理，所以附载于此。我以前患溏泻，每天天亮必去厕所，有人教我买豆附丸服用，马上好了，但其方子没有得到。后来再患此，只用姜煎附子加豆蔻，服后就好了。

The Ben Shi Fang says: a relative had one loose stool each dawn, around the fifth watch, for several months. Someone said this is called kidney diarrhea, caused by the kidney being affected by yin qi, and it was cured with Wuweizi San: wuweizi two liang and wuzhuyu half liang (the fine green-grained kind), both fried until fragrant and cooked, ground to powder, two qian each time with old rice water. Its discussion of loose diarrhea is reasonable, so it is appended. I once had loose diarrhea and went to the toilet every dawn; someone advised me to buy Doufu Wan and it cured me, but I could not get the formula. Another year it recurred and I took only ginger-decocted fuzi with added doukou, and recovered.

💡 五更泻即『鸡鸣泻』『肾泄』，传统归于命门火衰，后世四神丸（补骨脂、肉豆蔻、五味子、吴茱萸）正是由此发展。吴茱萸、附子辛热有毒，须按医嘱，不能自己配。今天要先排除慢性肠炎、乳糜泻、肠易激综合征、结肠病变。附子的剂量、炮制要求严格，学习时不要照原文自行用药。

痢餘見瀉

（标题）痢（其余见泄泻篇）。

(Heading) Dysentery (the rest is under diarrhea).

💡 痢疾相当于细菌性痢疾、阿米巴痢疾及炎症性肠病等引起的黏液脓血便，伴里急后重。今天需要实验室检查和规范治疗，急性重症需要补液和抗生素。

素問言泄痢有五種一曰胃泄飲食不化而色黄胃與脾合故黄也二曰脾泄腹脹而注泄無休又上逆嘔此為寒熱之患也三曰大腸泄食畢腸鳴切痛而痢白色大腸與肺合故白也四曰小腸泄身瘦而便膿血小腸與心合心主血也五曰大瘕泄裏急後重數至圊不能便莖中痛此腎泄也

《素问》说泄痢有五种：一是胃泄，饮食不化而大便色黄，胃与脾相合，所以色黄；二是脾泄，腹胀而下注泄泻不止，又上逆呕吐，这是寒热错杂的病患；三是大肠泄，吃完饭就肠鸣剧痛，泻出白色，大肠与肺相合，所以色白；四是小肠泄，身体消瘦而便脓血，小肠与心相合，心主血；五是大瘕泄，里急后重，频频去厕所却解不出，阴茎疼痛，这是肾泄。

The Suwen says there are five kinds of diarrhea and dysentery. First, stomach diarrhea: food is undigested and the stool is yellow; the stomach is paired with the spleen, hence yellow. Second, spleen diarrhea: abdominal distension with ceaseless downpouring diarrhea and also upward-rebelling vomiting; this is a disorder of cold and heat. Third, large intestine diarrhea: borborygmus and sharp pain right after eating, and white stool; the large intestine is paired with the lung, hence white. Fourth, small intestine diarrhea: emaciation with pus and blood in the stool; the small intestine is paired with the heart, which governs blood. Fifth, great conglomeration diarrhea: tenesmus, repeatedly going to the toilet without being able to pass stool, with penile pain; this is kidney diarrhea.

💡 这段实际出自《难经·五十七难》的五泄，作者说是《素问》，属于转引中的归属不准。五泄按脏腑配属解释症状：黄属脾土，白属肺金，血属心。大瘕泄的里急后重与痢疾一致，『茎中痛』是肾与膀胱、前阴相关的表现。五行配属是古代用来归类的理论工具，今天不能当成病原学。

諸家方有二十餘種此唯言五種葢舉其綱也必用方亦有赤白疳蠱之别其大槩則藏府寒也廪丘公所謂諸下悉寒是也

各家方书有二十多种分类，这里只说五种，是举其纲领。《必用方》也有赤白、疳蛊之分，大体上是脏腑虚寒，廪丘公所说的『凡是下利都属寒』就是这个意思。

Various schools list over twenty types, while this passage names only five, giving the outline. The Biyong Fang also distinguishes red and white dysentery and gan-gu types. In general it is cold of the zang-fu organs, as what Lord Linqiu meant by 'all downward discharges are cold.'

💡 『诸下悉寒』是一种倾向，并不等于所有痢疾都属寒；热痢、湿热痢也很常见。王执中偏重温补，这一点读者要有辨识力，不要忽略湿热、毒痢型。

數予治人痢惟與以鎮靈丹無有不效或未效更加丸數則效矣若蠱利則用栢葉黄連煎服見既效

我多次治疗痢疾，只给人服镇灵丹，没有不见效的，如果没效，加多丸数就见效了。如果是蛊痢，就用柏叶、黄连煎服，见《既效方》。

I have treated dysentery many times by giving only Zhenling pills, and without exception they worked; if they did not, increasing the number of pills did. For gu-dysentery, use a decoction of cypress leaf and huanglian, as in the Jixiao fang.

💡 『无有不效』是个人经验的夸大表述。『蛊利』大约是毒痢、重症脓血痢。黄连清热燥湿解毒，是治痢常用药，现代也确认其含小檗碱有抗菌作用。

諸痢惟耆域方用厚朴櫻粟殻末最佳後人又加木香黄連陳皮等分甘草拌之黄榖葉數片姜棗烏梅水煎予嘗用之驗故載于此

各种痢疾中，只有耆域的方子用厚朴、罂粟壳末最好。后人又加木香、黄连、陈皮等分，用甘草拌，再加黄谷叶几片、生姜、大枣、乌梅，水煎。我曾用过有效，所以载在这里。

Among all dysentery formulas, only Qi Yu's, using houpo and poppy husk powder, is best. Later people added muxiang, huanglian and chenpi in equal amounts, mixed with gancao, with a few leaves of huanggu, ginger, jujube and wumei, decocted in water. I have used it with proven effect, so I record it here.

💡 罂粟壳收涩止泻，含阿片类生物碱，今天属管制药物，只能在严格监管下使用，且急性感染性痢疾不宜过早收涩，可能使毒素滞留。木香、陈皮行气，黄连清热，乌梅酸涩，甘草调和。

然痢本無惡證而有患此而死者或者世醫以痢為熱病多服冷藥故也若其急難亦當灼艾不可專用藥云

然而痢疾本来没有严重的恶证，却有人患此病而死，或许是世上的医生把痢疾当成热病，多用寒凉药的缘故。如果病情紧急，也应该灼艾灸，不可只用药。

Yet dysentery by itself has no grave signs, and still some die of it, perhaps because physicians of the age regarded dysentery as a heat disease and gave too much cold medicine. In an emergency one should also burn moxa rather than rely on drugs alone.

💡 这是作者的看法，并不是定论。现代医学中，痢疾的死亡多与脱水、电解质紊乱、毒血症有关，补液是第一位的。灸法可作辅助，不能替代补液和抗生素。

復溜主腸澼便膿血泄痢後重腹痛如痓状千

复溜主治肠澼、便脓血、泄痢后重、腹痛如痉挛状（《千金》）。

Fuliu governs intestinal bi with pus and blood in the stool, diarrhea with tenesmus, and abdominal pain as if in spasm (the Qianjin).

💡 复溜KI7。『后重』即里急后重（tenesmus）。

交信主泄痢赤白銅同漏血

交信主治泄痢赤白（《铜人》同）、漏血。

Jiaoxin governs red and white diarrhea-dysentery (the Tongren agrees) and abnormal bleeding.

💡 交信KI8。『赤白』指带血的红痢和白色黏液的白痢。

太衝曲泉主溏泄痢注下血

太冲、曲泉主治溏泄、痢疾下注、下血。

Taichong and Ququan govern loose diarrhea, dysentery with downpour and blood in the stool.

💡 太冲LR3、曲泉LR8，肝经穴，与『肝藏血』相关。

小腸俞主泄痢膿血五色重下腫痛

小肠俞主治泄痢脓血、五色下利、后重肿痛。

Xiaochangshu governs diarrhea-dysentery with pus and blood, five-colored discharge, tenesmus, and swollen painful anus.

💡 小肠俞BL27。『五色』是各色混杂的黏液脓血便。

丹田主泄痢不禁小便絞痛關元

丹田主治泄痢不禁、小便绞痛；关元（也同样）。

Dantian governs uncontrolled diarrhea-dysentery and cramping urinary pain; Guanyuan likewise.

💡 丹田（见前）、关元CV4。原文断行把『泄痢』和『不禁』接起来。

大谿主泄痢不止脾俞主泄痢不食食不生肌

大溪（太溪）主治泄痢不止；脾俞主治泄痢、不能进食、吃了不长肌肉。

Daxi (Taixi) governs ceaseless diarrhea-dysentery; Pishu governs diarrhea-dysentery with inability to eat and with food failing to build flesh.

💡 太溪KI3、脾俞BL20。『食不生肌』是营养吸收不良的表现。

五樞主婦人赤白裏急瘈瘲

五枢主治妇人赤白带下、里急、抽搐。

Wushu governs red and white vaginal discharge, tenesmus and spasm in women.

💡 五枢GB27，带脉与足少阳交会。『赤白』在此多指带下。

曲泉治泄水下利膿血銅見風勞

曲泉治疗水泻、下痢脓血（《铜人》，见风劳篇）。

Ququan treats watery diarrhea and dysentery with pus and blood (the Tongren, see the chapter on wind-consumption).

💡 曲泉LR8。

中膂俞治腸冷赤白痢明同膀胱俞療泄痢腹痛明

中膂俞治疗肠冷、赤白痢（《明堂》同）；膀胱俞治疗泄痢、腹痛（《明堂》）。

Zhonglushu treats intestinal cold with red and white dysentery (the Mingtang agrees); Pangguangshu treats diarrhea-dysentery with abdominal pain (the Mingtang).

💡 中膂俞BL29、膀胱俞BL28。

脊俞療温病積聚下痢銅作下利

脊俞治疗温病、积聚、下痢（《铜人》写作『下利』）。

Jishu treats warm disease, accumulations and dysentery (the Tongren writes xiali).

💡 脊俞即脊中GV6。这里是文字异文说明。

關元療洩痢見便不禁

关元治疗泄痢（见大便不禁篇）。

Guanyuan treats diarrhea-dysentery (see the chapter on fecal incontinence).

💡 关元CV4。

小兒痢下赤白秋末脱肛每厠腹痛不可忍灸十二椎下節間名接脊穴一壮

小儿下痢赤白，秋末脱肛，每次上厕所腹痛不能忍，灸第十二椎下的节间，名接脊穴，一壮。

For children with red and white dysentery, rectal prolapse in late autumn, and unbearable abdominal pain at each defecation, moxa the interspace below the 12th vertebra, called the Jiejixue, one cone.

💡 『接脊穴』是奇穴，位置在第十二胸椎下棘突间，大致对应悬枢GV5或附近，原文并不用标准名。儿童施灸必须慎重，壮数要少，并注意防烫。小儿脱肛合并血便要就医排查。

黄帝療小兒疳痢脱肛體瘦渴飲形容瘦悴諸藥不差灸尾翠骨上三寸骨陷間三壮岐伯云三伏内用桃水浴孩子午正時當日灸之用青帛拭似見疳蟲隨汗出神效

黄帝治疗小儿疳痢、脱肛、身体消瘦、口渴多饮、面容憔悴，各种药都不好，灸尾骶骨上三寸骨陷之间三壮。岐伯说：在三伏天用桃枝水给孩子洗浴，正午时当天灸，用青布擦拭，似乎能见到疳虫随汗出，神效。

The Yellow Emperor treated infantile gan-dysentery with rectal prolapse, emaciation, thirst and drinking, and haggard appearance, when all medicines failed, by moxa at the depression 3 cun above the tail-bone, 3 cones. Qi Bo said: during the dog days, bathe the child in peach-wood water, moxa at exact noon on the same day, and wipe with green silk; it seems one sees 'gan worms' come out with the sweat, with marvelous effect.

💡 这是假托黄帝、岐伯的民间传方，不是《内经》原文。『疳』是小儿营养不良、消化不良的慢性病，包括寄生虫、营养缺乏等。『疳虫随汗出』属于古代想象，不能当作病原学事实。尾骶上三寸大致在腰骶部，对应腰阳关GV3或命门GV4一带（具体位置按原书）。『三伏灸』与后世冬病夏治的想法同源，但对小儿直接灸要慎重。

小兒伏深冷痢不止灸臍下二寸三寸間動脉中三壮

小儿深伏的冷痢，泻不止：灸脐下二寸、三寸之间动脉处，三壮。

For deep-seated cold dysentery in children that will not stop, moxa the pulsating spot between 2 and 3 cun below the navel, 3 cones.

💡 脐下二寸为石门CV5，三寸为关元CV4，两者之间约在这个区域。腹主动脉搏动处，实际操作要避免用力按压或直接大灸。

婦人水洩痢灸氣海百壮

妇人水泻痢：灸气海一百壮。

For watery diarrhea-dysentery in women, moxa Qihai, 100 cones.

💡 气海CV6，温补元气。

泄痢食不消不作肌膚灸脾俞隨年壮千

泄痢、食物不消化、不长肌肉：灸脾俞，壮数按年龄（《千金》）。

For diarrhea-dysentery with undigested food and failure to build flesh, moxa Pishu with cones equal to the patient's age (the Qianjin).

💡 脾俞BL20。

泄注五利便膿重下腹痛灸小腸俞百壮

泄泻下注、五种痢疾、便脓、后重、腹痛：灸小肠俞一百壮。

For downpour diarrhea, the five kinds of dysentery, pus in the stool, tenesmus and abdominal pain, moxa Xiaochangshu, 100 cones.

💡 小肠俞BL27。

泄痢不禁小腹絞痛灸石門百壮三報

泄痢不禁、小腹绞痛：灸石门一百壮，三次施行。

For uncontrolled diarrhea-dysentery with cramping lower abdominal pain, moxa Shimen, 100 cones, repeated three times.

💡 石门CV5。『三报』是分三次进行。女性用石门的传统禁忌见前注。

久痢百治不差灸足陽門下一寸高骨上陷中去大指岐三寸隨年又臍中二三百壮又關元三百十日

久痢百治不好：灸足阳（明）门下一寸、高骨之上的凹陷处，距大趾趾缝三寸，壮数按年龄；又灸脐中二三百壮；又灸关元三百壮，十天一次。

For chronic dysentery that no treatment cures, moxa 1 cun below the yang(ming) gate on the high bone's upper depression, 3 cun from the toe web of the big toe, with cones equal to the patient's age; also the navel 200 to 300 cones; also Guanyuan 300 cones, once in ten days.

💡 『足阳门』疑为『足阳明』之误，具体穴位按『去大指岐三寸』推断，约当太冲LR3一带，但无法肯定，原文有疑问，学习时需要对照《千金》。神阙CV8、关元CV4。二三百壮、三百壮是古代大剂量，今天不要照搬。

灸赤白下灸窮骨多為佳

灸治赤白痢，灸穷骨处，壮数多最好。

For red and white dysentery, moxa the coccygeal end; the more cones the better.

💡 『穷骨』即尾骨末端，约在长强GV1处。『多为佳』是古代强调刺激量，今天用灸要注意皮肤烫伤和感染。

四支不舉多汗洞痢灸大横隨年餘見千金

四肢无力抬不起、多汗、洞泄下痢：灸大横，壮数按年龄。其余见《千金》。

For limp limbs, profuse sweating and dong-diarrhea, moxa Daheng with cones equal to the patient's age. The rest is in the Qianjin.

💡 大横SP15，在脐旁4寸。

痢暴下如水云云氣海百壮指

（《千金》等）痢疾突然下泻如水，……灸气海一百壮。（出《指迷》）

For dysentery with sudden watery discharge..., moxa Qihai 100 cones (from the Zhimi).

💡 气海CV6。这一句是从《千金》类文献的转引，末字『指』是出处缩写，指《指迷》，与本书他处『指迷云』同。

便血餘見痢腸風

（标题）便血（其余见痢、肠风篇）。

(Heading) Blood in the stool (the rest is under dysentery and intestinal wind).

💡 便血相当于今天的消化道出血、痔、肠炎、息肉、肿瘤等。『肠风』是便血鲜红如溅、来势急的一种古代分类。任何不明原因便血必须就医做肠镜等检查。

復溜太衝等並見痢會陽見瀉主便血千下亷幽門明同

复溜、太冲等，都见痢篇；会阳（见泻篇）主治便血（《千金》）；下廉、幽门（《明堂》同）。

Fuliu, Taichong and others are all under dysentery; Huiyang (see the chapter on diarrhea) governs blood in the stool (the Qianjin); Xialian and Youmen (the Mingtang agrees).

💡 复溜KI7、太冲LR3、会阳BL35、下廉ST39、幽门KI21。这里以交叉引用为主。

太白見吐瀉治洩利膿血銅太白治吐洩膿血見腹脹

太白（见吐泻篇）治疗泄痢脓血（《铜人》）；太白治疗呕吐、泄泻、脓血（见腹胀篇）。

Taibai (see the chapter on vomiting and diarrhea) treats diarrhea-dysentery with pus and blood (the Tongren); Taibai treats vomiting, diarrhea and pus and blood (see the chapter on abdominal distension).

💡 太白SP3。

小腸俞治大便膿血出明同下髎治大便下血

小肠俞治疗大便脓血（《明堂》同）；下髎治疗大便下血。

Xiaochangshu treats pus and blood in the stool (the Mingtang agrees); Xialiao treats bloody stool.

💡 小肠俞BL27、下髎BL34。

腹哀治大便膿血見腹痛千又云寒中食不化腹痛

腹哀治疗大便脓血（见腹痛篇）；《千金》又说，主治中寒、食物不化、腹痛。

Fuai treats pus and blood in the stool (see the chapter on abdominal pain); the Qianjin also says it treats cold in the middle, undigested food and abdominal pain.

💡 腹哀SP16，位于脐上3寸旁开4寸。

勞宮見傷寒治大小便血

劳宫（见伤寒篇）治疗大小便出血。

Laogong (see the chapter on cold damage) treats bleeding in both urine and stool.

💡 劳宫PC8。心包经荥穴，泻心火，所以对热迫血行的出血有一定作用。

陸氏續集驗方治下血不止量臍心與脊骨平於脊骨上灸七壮即止如再發即再灸七壮永除根本目覩數人有效予嘗用之灸人腸風皆除根本神效無比然亦須按其骨突處痠疼方灸之不疼則不灸也但便血本因於腸風腸風即腸痔不可分而為三或分為三而治之非也

《陆氏续集验方》治疗下血不止：量取脐心，与脊骨在同一水平的位置，在脊骨上灸七壮就止；如果再发，再灸七壮，可以永远除根。我亲眼见过几个人有效，我也曾用此法灸人肠风，都除了根，神效无比。但必须按压那个骨突处，酸痛才灸，不疼就不灸。便血本来是由肠风引起，肠风就是肠痔，不能分成三种，或者分成三种来治，都是不对的。

Lu's Xuji Yan Fang treats unceasing bleeding of the lower body: measure the navel center and find the level on the spine at the same height; moxa on the spine 7 cones and the bleeding stops. If it recurs, moxa 7 cones again to eradicate it permanently. I have witnessed several persons for whom it worked, and I have used it on people with intestinal wind and all were eradicated, with incomparable divine effect. But one must press the bony prominence and moxa only if it is sore and aching; if it does not ache, do not moxa. Blood in the stool originates from intestinal wind, and intestinal wind is the same as intestinal hemorrhoid; it should not be divided into three, and treating it as three is wrong.

💡 与脐同高的脊柱处约在第二腰椎棘突下，即命门GV4。这是『按压有压痛点才灸』的阿是穴思路。『除根』『神效』是古代夸饰，现代医学里便血原因多样，包括痔、肛裂、息肉、炎症性肠病、结直肠癌等，不能凭灸一次而放心，尤其是40岁以上或反复便血者要做肠镜。作者的『肠风即肠痔』是他个人的看法，后世仍分肠风、脏毒、痔血等。

痔瘻漏餘見瘍瘻

（标题）痔、瘘、漏（其余见疡瘘篇）。

(Heading) Hemorrhoids, fistula and sinus (the rest is under sores and fistulae).

💡 此为下一节的标题，正文不在所给原文之内。痔瘘相当于今天的痔疮、肛瘘，针灸常取承山BL57、长强GV1、二白EX-UE2等。

長强治腸風下血五種痔疳蝕下部3634此痔根本是冷謹冷食房勞銅與明同明下云療久痔會隂治榖道瘙擾久痔相通者死千云主痔與隂相通者死會陽治久痔小腸俞治五痔疼明同秩邊治五痔發腫復溜治血痔洩後腫飛揚治野雞痔承山治久痔腫痛扶承治久痔尻脽腫大便難隂胞有寒小便不利千云療五種痔瀉鮮血尻脽中腫大便難小便不利氣海俞療痔病瀉血明飛揚主痔篡傷痛商丘復溜主痔血泄後重勞宮主熱痔承筋承扶委中陽谷主痔痛掖下腫商丘主痔骨蝕銅云痔疾骨疽蝕支溝章門主馬刀腫瘻絶骨主瘻馬刀掖腫俠谿陽輔銅同太衝主掖下腫馬刀瘻銅云太衝臨泣治馬刀瘍瘻天突章門天池支溝王漏天突天牕主漏頸痛長强療下漏明見痔一千用亭厯子豉作餅灸漏外臺云不可灸頭瘡亭厯氣入腦殺人灸痔法疾若未深尾閭骨下近榖道灸一穴便可除去如傳信方先以經年槐枝煎湯洗後灸其上七壮大稱其驗如本草只以馬藍菜根一握水三碗煎碗半乘熱以小口瓦器中熏洗令腫退於元生鼠妳根上灸即不可灸尖頭恐效遲如患深用湯洗未退易湯洗令消然後灸覺火氣通至胷乃效病雖深至二十餘壮永絶根本以竹片䕶四邊肉仍於天色寒凉時灸忌毒物集效

长强穴主治肠风便血、五种痔疮、疳疮蚀烂下部（原文'3634'为底本缺字）。这类痔疮的根本原因是受寒，应当谨慎避免冷食和房劳过度。《铜人》与《明堂》记载相同，《明堂》下文说治疗久痔。会阴穴主治肛门瘙痒、久痔，若痔瘘与前阴相通者多属危重难治（《千金》说主治痔疮与前阴相通者死）。会阳穴主治久痔。小肠俞主治五痔疼痛，《明堂》记载相同。秩边穴主治五痔发肿。复溜穴主治血痔、便后肿痛。飞扬穴主治野鸡痔（一种瘘管性痔疾）。承山穴主治久痔肿痛。扶承（即承扶）穴主治久痔、臀部肿胀、大便难、阴胞有寒、小便不利；《千金》说治疗五种痔疮、泻鲜血、臀部中肿胀、大便难、小便不利。气海俞主治痔病泻血，《明堂》记载相同。飞扬穴主治痔疮、会阴部损伤疼痛。商丘、复溜主治痔血、便后里急后重。劳宫穴主治热痔。承筋、承扶、委中、阳谷主治痔疮疼痛、腋下肿胀。商丘穴主治痔疮蚀骨，《铜人》说痔疾、骨疽蚀烂。支沟、章门主治马刀（瘰疬）肿胀成瘘。绝骨（悬钟）主治瘘疮、马刀、腋下肿胀。侠溪、阳辅主治同上，《铜人》记载相同。太冲穴主治腋下肿胀、马刀成瘘；《铜人》说太冲、临泣治疗马刀疮瘘。天突、章门、天池、支沟主治'王漏'（疑为'玉漏'或特定瘘管名称）。天突、天窗主治瘘疮、颈痛。长强穴治疗下漏，详见'痔'条。一说《千金方》用葶苈子、豆豉做成饼来艾灸瘘疮；《外台秘要》说不可艾灸头部疮疡，恐葶苈之气入脑致命。艾灸治痔法：如果病情还不深，在尾闾骨下靠近肛门处灸一穴即可消除。如《传信方》记载，先用陈年槐枝煎汤清洗，再在其上灸七壮，功效显著。如《本草》记载，只用马蓝菜根一把，加水三碗煎至一碗半，趁热用小口瓦器熏洗，使肿消退，在原本生长的鼠奶（痔核，古称鼠乳）的根部上灸，但不可灸尖端，恐怕见效慢。如果患处较深，用汤洗未退就换汤再洗令其消散，然后施灸，自觉火气通至胸部才有效；病虽深，灸至二十多壮可永绝根本。用竹片护住周围皮肉，仍在天气寒凉时施灸，忌食发物（毒物），引自《集效方》。

Changqiang (GV 1) treats intestinal wind with hematochezia, five types of hemorrhoids, and gangrenous erosion of the lower body (a character is missing in the source here). The root cause of such hemorrhoids is cold; one must be cautious of cold food and excessive sexual activity. The Copper Man and Mingtang agree, and the Mingtang further notes treating chronic hemorrhoids. Huiyin (CV 1) treats anal itching and chronic hemorrhoids; when a fistula communicates with the anterior yin (genitalia), it is fatal (the Qianjin states that hemorrhoids communicating with the yin are fatal). Huiyang (BL 35) treats chronic hemorrhoids. Xiaochangshu (BL 27) treats pain of the five hemorrhoids, agreeing with the Mingtang. Zhibian (BL 54) treats swelling from the five hemorrhoids. Fuliu (KI 7) treats bleeding hemorrhoids and post-defecation swelling. Feiyang (BL 58) treats 'wild chicken hemorrhoids' (a type of anal fistula). Chengshan (BL 57) treats swelling and pain of chronic hemorrhoids. Fucheng (Chengfu, BL 36) treats chronic hemorrhoids, gluteal swelling, difficult defecation, cold in the yin chamber (lower abdomen/pelvis), and difficult urination; the Qianjin states it treats five types of hemorrhoids, passing fresh blood, swelling in the buttocks, difficult defecation, and difficult urination. Qihaishu (BL 24) treats hemorrhoidal bleeding, agreeing with the Mingtang. Feiyang (BL 58) treats hemorrhoids and perineal/anal injury pain. Shangqiu (SP 5) and Fuliu (KI 7) treat hemorrhoidal bleeding and tenesmus after defecation. Laogong (PC 8) treats heat hemorrhoids. Chengjin (BL 56), Chengfu (BL 36), Weizhong (BL 40), and Yanggu (SI 5) treat hemorrhoidal pain and axillary swelling. Shangqiu (SP 5) treats hemorrhoids eroding bone; the Copper Man says hemorrhoidal disease and bone gangrene/erosion. Zhigou (TE6) and Zhangmen (LR 13) treat scrofulous swelling and fistulas ('madou'). Juegu (Xuanzhong, GB 39) treats fistulas, scrofula, and axillary swelling. Xiaxi (GB 43) and Yangfu (GB 38) treat the same, agreeing with the Copper Man. Taichong (LR 3) treats axillary swelling and scrofulous fistulas; the Copper Man says Taichong (LR 3) and Linqi (GB 41) treat scrofulous ulcer-fistulas. Tiantu (CV 22), Zhangmen (LR 13), Tianchi (PC 1), and Zhigou (TE6) treat 'king/jade fistula' ('wanglou'). Tiantu (CV 22) and Tianchuang (SI 16) treat fistulas and neck pain. Changqiang (GV 1) treats lower fistulas; see also 'Hemorrhoids.' One source says the Qianjin uses Tinglizi (Semen Lepidii) and fermented soybeans (chi) made into cakes to moxibust fistulas; the Waitai warns not to moxibust head sores lest the vapor of Tinglizi enter the brain and kill. Method for moxibusting hemorrhoids: if the disease is not yet deep, moxibust one point below the Weilü (coccyx) bone near the anus to remove it. According to the Chuanxin Fang, first wash with a decoction of year-old sophora twigs (huai zhi), then moxibust seven cones on it, which is highly praised for efficacy. According to the Bencao, take one handful of malan root, decoct three bowls of water down to one and a half bowls, and while hot steam and wash in a narrow-mouthed earthenware vessel until swelling subsides, then moxibust on the original/growing shuna root (do not moxibust the pointed tip lest effectiveness be delayed). If deep, wash with decoction, and if swelling does not subside, change the decoction and wash until resolved, then moxibust until the fire qi is felt reaching the chest for effect; even if deep, up to twenty-odd cones permanently eradicates the root. Protect the surrounding flesh with bamboo splints, still moxibust in cool/cold weather, and avoid 'toxic' (trigger/irritating) foods, from Jixiao Fang.

💡 本段汇总了痔疮、肛瘘、瘰疬（马刀）及部分便血的针灸与外治法，体现了宋代文献对相关病证的混杂归类。涉及穴位：长强 GV1、会阴 CV1、会阳 BL35、小肠俞 BL27、秩边 BL54、复溜 KI7、飞扬 BL58、承山 BL57、承扶 BL36、气海俞 BL24、商丘 SP5、劳宫 PC8、承筋 BL56、委中 BL40、阳谷 SI5、支沟 TE6、章门 LR13、悬钟 GB39、侠溪 GB43、阳辅 GB38、太冲 LR3、足临泣 GB41、天突 CV22、天窗 SI16、天池 PC1。古代理论认为'痔瘘相通者死'，反映了古代缺乏有效根治复杂肛瘘、高位瘘及合并严重感染/全身消耗时的高病死率；'火气通至胸'属于古代得气感传的经验描述。现代医学对照：痔疮、肛瘘、肛周脓肿、直肠息肉/肿瘤出血、结核性或非特异性淋巴结炎（瘰疬/马刀）、颈部或会阴窦道/瘘管。临床与考试意义：承山、长强、会阳、秩边、二白（他篇常见）等是治疗痔瘘的常用穴，但现代临床对肛瘘多以手术或挂线为主，艾灸多用于术后辅助缓解疼痛、促进局部循环或轻浅痔病；在肛周直接施灸需注意防烫伤与感染扩散，'痔瘘相通者死'在现代已不适用，但提示复杂瘘管病情较重、需专科评估。

千金灸漏更有數穴

《千金方》中艾灸瘘疮还有其他几个穴位。

The Qianjin has several additional acupoints for moxibusting fistulas.

💡 承上启下的引文句，提示下文或相关篇目补充《千金要方》治瘘穴位，原文此处较简略。临床与现代对照：瘘管（如肛瘘、颈部淋巴结结核破溃成窦道/瘘管）在现代需明确原发病灶与引流路径，单纯针灸难以闭合深在瘘管，仅可作辅助调理。

腸風

肠风（中医病名，指大便下血，色鲜红或夹血块，多因风邪或湿热、积热客于肠络所致）。

Changfeng ('intestinal wind,' a TCM pattern/disease characterized by hematochezia—fresh bright red blood or blood clots in the stool—traditionally attributed to wind, damp-heat, or accumulated heat invading the intestinal collaterals).

💡 中医病证标题。'肠风'在古代常与'脏毒'、'肠痔'相联系，指便血。现代医学对照：下消化道出血常见病因，如内痔、肛裂、直肠/结肠息肉、溃疡性结肠炎、克罗恩病、结直肠癌或血管畸形等。临床与考试意义：考试中常考肠风便血与脏毒便血的鉴别（一般以血色鲜红、量较多、便前或便时下血、常伴有风热或肠络受损表现为特征，但各家略有差异）；临床遇便血首重鉴别器质性病变，切勿仅靠针灸延误诊断。

脊端窮骨脊骨盡處名1841尾當中灸三壮治腸風瀉血即愈須顛倒身方灸得久冷五痔便血脊中百壮千翼何敎授湯簿有此疾積年皆一灸除根湯簿因傳此法後觀灸經此穴療小兒脱肛瀉血葢岐伯灸小兒法也後人因之以灸大人腸風瀉血爾葢大人小兒之病初不異故也五痔便血失屎廻氣百壮在脊窮骨上赤白下

脊柱末端称为'穷骨'，脊骨尽处名叫“1841尾”（1841为底本缺字，疑为“龟”，长强古名龟尾，即尾骨端），在正中灸三壮，治疗肠风泻血即可痊愈，必须俯卧或倒身体位才能施灸。久冷导致的五痔便血，在脊中穴灸百壮。（《千金翼方》）。何教授、汤主簿都患这病多年，都是一灸除根，汤主簿因此传开这个方法；后来查阅《灸经》，见此穴治疗小儿脱肛泻血，大概是岐伯灸治小儿的方法，后人沿用它来灸治成人肠风泻血，因为成人与小儿的疾病本源并无不同。五痔便血、失禁（失屎），回气穴灸百壮，穴在脊柱穷骨之上，主治赤白下痢。

The end of the spine is called the 'qiong gu' (terminal/coccygeal bone), where the spinal bone ends, named '... tail' (a character is missing; probably guiwei, 'tortoise tail', an old name of Changqiang at the tip of the coccyx); moxibusting three cones in the center cures intestinal wind with bloody diarrhea, and one must invert or position the body properly to moxibust it. For chronic cold causing five types of hemorrhoids with hematochezia, moxibust one hundred cones at Jizhong (GV 6). (Qianjin Yi.) Professor He and Registrar Tang suffered from this disease for years and both had the root cured with a single moxibustion session, so Tang passed down this method; later consulting the Moxibustion Classic (Jiu Jing), this point was found to treat infantile rectal prolapse with bloody diarrhea, which was likely Qibo's method for infants, later adapted by descendants to moxibust adult intestinal wind with bloody diarrhea because adult and pediatric illnesses do not differ at their origin. For five types of hemorrhoids with hematochezia and fecal incontinence ('loss of stool'), moxibust Huiqi for one hundred cones, located above the qiong gu of the spine, treating red and white dysentery.

💡 本段涉及脊中 GV6及经外奇穴/古穴名（穷骨/尾闾、回气；'1841尾'为古籍传抄刻版错讹）。涉及穴位：脊中 GV6。古代理论认为'大人小儿之病初不异'，体现了古代对同类病机（如中气下陷、肠络受损、下焦虚寒）的推演；但'百壮'在现代临床已属极重灸量，易致局部严重灼伤甚至感染，现代仅作历史参考，临床多采用温和灸或较少壮数。现代医学对照：下消化道出血、内痔出血、直肠脱垂伴黏膜出血、肛门括约肌松弛/大便失禁、感染性或炎性肠病。考试与临床意义：脊中穴位于第11胸椎棘突下，现代常用于腰背痛、癫痫、小儿惊风等，治便血、脱肛多见于古籍文献题。

長强治腸風下血銅見痔

长强穴主治肠风便血，《铜人》记载详见'痔'条。

Changqiang (GV 1) treats intestinal wind with hematochezia; the Copper Man refers to 'Hemorrhoids.'

💡 涉及穴位：长强 GV1（位于会阴部，尾骨下端与肛门连线的中点）。古代理论与现代医学对照：长强属督脉络穴，局部位于阴部神经、肛提肌及直肠下段附近，针刺或温和灸可调节盆底肌张力与局部血供，现代常用于痔疮、脱肛、便秘、便血及腰骶部病症。临床与考试意义：针刺长强一般要求斜刺（紧靠尾骨前方向上刺入），避免误伤直肠；临床多用毫针或温和灸，少用直接瘢痕灸。

腸風藥甚衆多不作效何也本草衍義曰腸風乃腸痔茍知其為痔而治之無不效矣若灸腸風長强為要穴云近李倉腸風市醫以杖量臍中於脊骨當臍處灸即愈予因此為人灸腸風皆除根陸氏方治下血除根

治疗肠风的药物很多却往往无效，是什么原因呢？《本草衍义》说：'肠风其实就是肠痔，如果认识到它是痔疮而加以治疗，就没有不效的。'如果用艾灸治疗肠风，长强是关键穴位。又说近来李仓患肠风，街市医生用杖量取脐水平线，在脊骨正对肚脐处施灸即愈；我因此替人灸治肠风也都除根。还有陆氏方治疗便血除根。

Why are there so many remedies for intestinal wind yet often no effect? The Bencao Yanyi states: 'Intestinal wind is actually intestinal hemorrhoids; if one recognizes it as hemorrhoids and treats it accordingly, there is nothing ineffective.' If moxibusting intestinal wind, Changqiang (GV 1) is the key point. It is also said that recently Li Cang had intestinal wind, and a street physician measured the level of the navel with a stick and moxibusted the spine directly opposite the navel, curing it; following this, I moxibusted others for intestinal wind and all had the root cured. There is also Lu's formula for curing hematochezia at the root.

💡 涉及穴位：长强 GV1；脊骨当脐处大致对应命门 GV4 或腰阳关 GV3 附近（古代量法与现代骨度分寸略有差异）。古代理论认为'肠风乃肠痔'，指出了便血与痔疮的密切关联，但现代医学明确便血原因远不止痔疮。现代医学对照：痔疮出血与结直肠息肉、炎症性肠病、憩室出血、结直肠癌等鉴别至关重要。临床与考试意义：体现了中医'同病异治/异病同治'的辨证思路，但临床绝不能把所有便血简单等同于痔疮而漏诊肿瘤；针灸临床中命门、长强等可用于虚寒性便血或慢性腹泻的辅助调理。

腸澼

肠澼（中医病名，指大便下脓血、里急后重，多与痢疾及湿热、寒湿积滞于肠有关）。

Changpi (a TCM disease/pattern characterized by stools with pus and blood and tenesmus, closely related to dysentery and damp-heat or cold-damp accumulation in the intestines).

💡 中医病证标题。'肠澼'见于《内经》《金匮要略》等古籍，核心表现为下痢脓血或赤白黏液便伴里急后重。现代医学对照：细菌性痢疾、阿米巴痢疾、溃疡性结肠炎、感染性肠炎等。临床与考试意义：考试中常考肠澼的病名释义与病机（湿热、寒湿、食积等阻滞肠腑，气血凝滞）；现代临床对感染性痢疾需及时规范抗感染与补液支持，针灸仅作辅助缓解腹痛、里急后重之用。

復溜見痢束骨會陽見瀉主腸澼千中都治腸澼㿉疝小腹痛銅四滿治腸澼切痛見積聚結積留飲澼囊胷滿飲食不消灸通谷五十壮千大腸俞主風腹中雷鳴大腸灌沸腸澼洩痢食不消化小腹絞痛腰脊疼强大小便難不能飲食灸百壮三報之諸結積留飲澼囊胷滿飲食不消通谷五十壮又胃管三百三報之第十五椎名下極俞主腹中疾腰痛膀胱寒澼飲注下隨年壮千翼會陽主腹中有寒泄注腸澼便血束骨主腸澼泄膺牕主腸鳴泄注陽綱主大便不節小便赤黄腸鳴泄注三焦俞小腸俞下窌意舎章門主腸鳴腹脹欲泄注千

复溜穴详见'痢'条；束骨、会阳详见'泻'条，主治肠澼。《千金方》记载，中都穴治疗肠澼、颓疝、小腹痛。《铜人》记载，四满穴治疗肠澼剧痛，详见'积聚'条。结积、留饮、澼囊、胸满、饮食不化，灸足通谷五十壮。《千金方》记载，大肠俞主治风邪、腹中雷鸣、大肠灌沸、肠澼泄痢、饮食不消化、小腹绞痛、腰脊拘急疼痛、大小便难、不能饮食，灸百壮并重复施灸三次（'三报之'）。各种结积、留饮、澼囊、胸满、饮食不化，灸通谷五十壮，又灸胃管（中脘）三百壮并重复三次。第十五椎下名下极俞（即悬枢附近/古奇穴名），主治腹中疾病、腰痛、膀胱寒、澼饮下泄，按年龄壮数施灸。《千金翼方》记载，会阳穴主治腹中有寒、泄注、肠澼便血。束骨穴主治肠澼泄泻。膺窗穴主治肠鸣泄注。阳纲穴主治大便失节、小便赤黄、肠鸣泄注。三焦俞、小肠俞、下髎、意舍、章门主治肠鸣、腹胀、欲泄注，引自《千金方》。

Fuliu (KI 7) is detailed under 'Dysentery'; Shugu (BL 65) and Huiyang (BL 35) are detailed under 'Diarrhea,' treating changpi. The Qianjin notes that Zhongdu (LR 6) treats changpi, tui-hernia, and lower abdominal pain. The Copper Man notes that Siman (KI 14) treats sharp pain of changpi, detailed under 'Accumulations.' For concretions/accumulations, retained fluids, pi-sac, chest fullness, and undigested food/drink, moxibust Tonggu (BL 66) for fifty cones. The Qianjin states that Dachangshu (BL 25) treats wind, borborygmus ('thunder in the abdomen'), gurgling/boiling in the large intestine, changpi with diarrhea/dysentery, undigested food, colicky lower abdominal pain, lumbar/spinal stiffness and pain, difficult urination and defecation, and inability to eat or drink; moxibust one hundred cones and repeat three times ('san bao zhi'). For various accumulations, retained fluids, pi-sac, chest fullness, and undigested food, moxibust Tonggu (BL 66) for fifty cones, and also moxibust Weiguan (Zhongwan, CV 12) for three hundred cones and repeat three times. Below the fifteenth vertebra is named Xiajishu (an ancient/extra point near XuanShu, GV 5), treating abdominal disorders, low back pain, bladder cold, and downward pouring of pi-fluid, moxibusted according to age in cones. The Qianjin Yi states that Huiyang (BL 35) treats cold in the abdomen, watery diarrhea, and changpi with hematochezia. Shugu (BL 65) treats changpi with diarrhea. Yingchuang (ST 16) treats borborygmus and watery diarrhea. Yanggang (BL 48) treats irregular defecation, dark-yellow urine, borborygmus, and watery diarrhea. Sanjiaoshu (BL 22), Xiaochangshu (BL 27), Xialiao (BL 34), Yishe (BL 49), and Zhangmen (LR 13) treat borborygmus, abdominal distension, and urgency of watery diarrhea, from the Qianjin.

💡 涉及穴位：复溜 KI7、束骨 BL65、会阳 BL35、中都 LR6、四满 KI14、足通谷 BL66、大肠俞 BL25、中脘 CV12、阳纲 BL48、三焦俞 BL22、小肠俞 BL27、下髎 BL34、意舍 BL49、章门 LR13、膺窗 ST16（下极俞为古籍穴名，大致在脊柱腰段第15椎附近，现代标准经穴无同名穴）。古代理论中'澼囊'、'留饮'指水饮停聚或肠胃积滞，'三报之'指重复灸疗，'百壮''三百壮'属古代重灸法，现代临床因烫伤风险极高已不主张如此大壮数，多改用温和灸、隔物灸或针刺。现代医学对照：急慢性肠炎、痢疾、肠易激综合征、腹部包块/肠梗阻先兆、腹腔积液或消化不良。临床与考试意义：大肠俞、中脘、章门、下髎等是调理胃肠气机与腹痛泄泻的要穴，考试中需注意区分各穴的归经与主治侧重。

腸痛餘見腸澼

肠痛（腹痛偏于肠部者），其余内容详见'肠澼'条。

Changtong (intestinal/lower abdominal pain); remaining details are found under 'Changpi.'

💡 病证标题与索引提示。'肠痛'在中医多指脐周或小腹、少腹部位的疼痛，与寒凝、气滞、血瘀、湿热或肠痈等有关。现代医学对照：急慢性肠炎、肠痉挛、阑尾炎（肠痈）、肠梗阻、盆腔或泌尿生殖系牵涉痛等。临床与考试意义：急性剧烈肠痛需警惕急腹症（如阑尾炎、肠梗阻、穿孔），切忌盲目艾灸或延误外科评估。

太白主腸痛甲見腸鳴陷谷等主腸痛千見腸鳴商曲治腸切痛銅見積聚建里療腸中疼嘔逆上氣心痛身腫明氣衝治腸中之熱銅見上氣

太白穴主治肠痛，《甲乙经》记载详见'肠鸣'条。陷谷等穴主治肠痛，《千金方》记载详见'肠鸣'条。商曲穴治疗肠部剧痛（切痛），详见'积聚'条。建里穴治疗肠中疼痛、呕逆、气上冲、心痛、身体浮肿，《明堂》记载相同。气冲（气街）穴治疗肠中热邪，《铜人》记载详见'上气'条。

Taibai (SP 3) treats intestinal pain; the Jiayi Jing refers to 'Borborygmus.' Xiangu (ST 43) and other points treat intestinal pain; the Qianjin refers to 'Borborygmus.' Shangqu (KI 17) treats sharp/cutting intestinal pain, detailed under 'Accumulations.' Jianli (CV 11) treats intestinal pain, nausea/retching, qi ascending counterflow, heart pain, and body edema, agreeing with the Mingtang. Qichong (Qijie, ST 30) treats heat in the intestines; the Copper Man refers to 'Ascending Qi.'

💡 涉及穴位：太白 SP3、陷谷 ST43、商曲 KI17、建里 CV11、气冲 ST30。古代理论中太白为脾经原穴、输穴，商曲为肾经穴位临近腹部，建里位于上腹任脉，气冲位于腹股沟上方胃经，均体现了'经脉所过，主治所及'与脏腑相关。现代医学对照：胃肠痉挛、功能性腹痛、消化不良、腹胀痛、腹股沟或下腹不适。临床与考试意义：太白、陷谷、建里、气冲等均为针灸学常考经穴，临床常用于脾胃肠腑气滞或寒热引起的腹痛、腹胀。

腸痛亦多端若疼甚者乃腸癰急宜服内補十全散等藥其他宜隨證灸之有老嫗大腸中常若裏急後重甚苦之自言人必無老新婦此竒疾也為按其大腸俞疼甚令歸灸之而愈

肠痛的原因也很复杂。如果疼痛特别剧烈，那就是'肠痈'（相当于现代阑尾炎或肠内化脓性炎症），应当抓紧服用内补十全散等药物；其他情况则应随证施灸。有一位老妇人，常觉大肠内好像有里急后重之感，十分痛苦，自己说世上必定没有像她这样老的新媳妇，认为这是奇疾。我替她按压大肠俞，痛得厉害，便让她回家灸这个穴，结果痊愈。

Intestinal pain also has many causes. If the pain is extremely severe, it is 'changyong' (intestinal abscess, corresponding to appendicitis or suppurative intestinal inflammation), and one should promptly take formulas such as Neibu Shiquan Wan/San; other cases should be moxibusted according to pattern differentiation. An elderly woman constantly felt as if there were tenesmus in her large intestine and suffered greatly, saying to herself there could be no old bride like her and considering it a strange illness. I pressed her Dachangshu (BL 25), which was very tender, and told her to go home and moxibust it, and she recovered.

💡 涉及穴位：大肠俞 BL25。'疼甚者乃肠痈'体现了古代对急腹症/化脓性肠病的警觉，但现代医学认为急性阑尾炎等急腹症以手术或规范抗感染治疗为主，内补十全散偏于气血双补，并非急性化脓期的首选。'按其大肠俞疼甚'是古代'以痛为输/循经取穴压痛点'的临床经验，现代针灸仍常通过背俞穴或募穴压痛辅助判断脏腑病变。临床与考试意义：考试中常考肠痈的病机（湿热蕴结、气滞血瘀、热毒化脓）及鉴别，临床遇剧烈腹痛必须先排除急腹症。

腸癰為病小腸重小便數似淋或繞臍生瘡或膿從臍出大便出膿血屈兩肘正灸肘頭鋭骨各百壮則下膿血止差

肠痈发病时，可见小腹/肠部坠重感、小便频数类似淋证，或绕脐生疮，或脓液从脐部流出，或大便排出脓血。将两肘屈曲，正对肘尖突出的骨端（即尺骨鹰嘴尖/天井或肘尖穴）各灸一百壮，那么下脓血就会停止而痊愈。

When changyong manifests, there may be heaviness in the lower abdomen/intestines, frequent urination resembling lin-strangury, sores around the umbilicus, pus discharging from the umbilicus, or pus and blood in the stool. Flex both elbows and moxibust the prominent bony tip of each elbow (the olecranon tip/elbow tip) with one hundred cones each, whereupon passing pus and blood will stop and recovery will follow.

💡 涉及穴位：肘尖（经外奇穴，位于屈肘时尺骨鹰嘴尖端；附近有手少阳三焦经的天井 TE10）。古代理论认为肠痈可出现绕脐生疮、溃脓或便脓血，反映肠内脓肿破溃或腹腔/脐周感染扩散；'各百壮'属古代重灸，现代临床极少采用。现代医学对照：急性或亚急性阑尾炎、阑尾周围脓肿穿孔、克罗恩病瘘管/脓肿、结直肠癌破溃感染或脐尿管/脐部感染。临床与考试意义：肘尖穴在古籍中常用于瘰疬、肠痈等热毒痈肿，现代医学强调此类情况需及时外科评估与抗生素治疗，不可仅依赖艾灸。

胡權内補十全散治腸癰神效

胡权用内补十全散治疗肠痈，疗效显著。

Hu Quan achieved remarkable results treating changyong with Neibu Shiquan San.

💡 方药医案/经验记载。内补十全散（即十全大补汤类方）由四君子汤、四物汤加黄芪、肉桂组成，主治气血两虚证；用于肠痈多见于病程较久、正气虚弱或溃后气血不足者，急性实热化脓期单用补益药并不恰当。现代医学对照：慢性消耗状态、术后恢复期或贫血/低蛋白血症的辅助调理，不能替代急性感染控制与外科处理。临床与考试意义：注意区分'急则治其标、热毒重者清热解毒排脓'与'虚者扶正补虚'的治则差异。

腸鳴腹鳴

肠鸣、腹鸣（指腹中肠管蠕动时发出咕噜声、水声或如雷鸣般的声响）。

Changming and fuming ('intestinal rumbling' and 'abdominal rumbling,' referring to gurgling, splashing water sounds, or thunder-like noises arising from intestinal movement).

💡 症状/病证标题。肠鸣、腹鸣在中医多与脾胃虚寒、肝气乘脾、湿浊内停或气滞肠腑有关。现代医学对照：肠鸣音亢进或活跃，可见于急性胃肠炎、消化不良、肠易激综合征、乳糖不耐受或机械性肠梗阻早期等。临床与考试意义：针灸考试中常考肠鸣的常见治穴（如天枢、足三里、上廉、阴陵泉等），临床可作为调理脾胃气机、观察胃肠功能恢复的常见指征。

不容治腹虚鳴銅見痃癖三間主胷滿腸鳴千胃俞主腹滿而鳴明下云腹中鳴臍中主腸中常鳴上衝於心天樞主腹脹腸鳴氣上衝胷又主婦人隂都主心滿氣逆腸鳴太白公孫大腸俞三焦俞等見瀉主腸鳴隂交主腸鳴濯濯有如水聲上亷主腸鳴相追逐漏谷主腸鳴强欠心悲氣逆膺牕主腸鳴泄注陷谷温溜漏谷復溜陽綱主腸鳴而痛下窌主婦人腸鳴注泄胷脇脹腸鳴切痛太白主之甲三里見胃三間京門見瀉關門見積氣三隂交見腹脹陷谷一水分神闕並見水腫承滿温溜三焦俞大腸俞胃俞腹脹天樞月事治腸鳴銅章門治腸鳴盈盈然千同食不化脅痛不得卧煩熱口乾不嗜食胷脇支滿喘息心痛腰下經有背脅痛不得轉側上亷治腸鳴氣走疰痛商丘治腹脹腸鳴不便脾虚令人不樂身寒善太息

不容穴治疗腹部虚性肠鸣，《铜人》记载详见'痃癖'条。三间穴主治胸满、肠鸣，《千金方》记载相同。胃俞主治腹满而鸣，《明堂》下文说腹中鸣。脐中（神阙）主治肠中常鸣、气上冲于心。天枢穴主治腹胀、肠鸣、气上冲胸，又主治妇人病。阴都穴主治心满、气逆、肠鸣。太白、公孙、大肠俞、三焦俞等穴，详见'泻'条，主治肠鸣。阴交穴主治肠鸣，咕噜作响如水声。上廉穴主治肠鸣如声响相追逐。漏谷穴主治肠鸣、频频打哈欠（强欠）、心中悲伤、气逆。膺窗穴主治肠鸣、水样泄泻。陷谷、温溜、漏谷、复溜、阳纲主治肠鸣而痛。下髎穴主治妇人肠鸣、水样泄泻。胸胁胀满、肠鸣剧痛，太白穴主治之，《甲乙经》记载相同。足三里详见'胃'条；三间、京门详见'泻'条；关门详见'积气'条；三阴交详见'腹胀'条；陷谷、水分、神阙均详见'水肿'条；承满、温溜、三焦俞、大肠俞、胃俞详见'腹胀'条。天枢（见'月事'条）治疗肠鸣（《铜人》）。章门穴治疗肠鸣、水声盈盈然，《千金方》记载相同，并有饮食不化、胁痛不得卧、烦热、口干、不嗜食、胸胁支满、喘息、心痛等症；《下经》（或《明堂下经》）记载有背胁痛不得转侧。上廉穴治疗肠鸣、气走窜作痛。商丘穴治疗腹胀、肠鸣、大便不畅，以及脾虚令人不乐、身寒、善太息。

Burong (ST 19) treats empty/deficient abdominal rumbling; the Copper Man refers to 'Xuanpi.' Sanjian (LI 3) treats chest fullness and borborygmus, agreeing with the Qianjin. Weishu (BL 21) treats abdominal fullness with rumbling; the Mingtang below says rumbling in the abdomen. Qizhong (Shenque, CV 8) treats frequent intestinal rumbling surging up toward the heart. Yinjiao (CV 7) treats borborygmus with splashing water sounds ('zhuozhuo'). Shanglian (LI 9) treats borborygmus chasing one another. Zusanli (ST 36) is under 'Stomach'; Sanjian (LI 3) and Jingmen (GB 25) are under 'Diarrhea'; Guanmen (ST 22) is under 'Accumulated Qi'; Sanyinjiao (SP 6) is under 'Abdominal Distension'; Xiangu (ST 43), Shuifen (CV 9), and Shenque (CV 8) are all under 'Edema'; Chengman (ST 20), Wenliu (LI 7), Sanjiaoshu (BL 22), Dachangshu (BL 25), and Weishu (BL 21) are under 'Abdominal Distension.'
