GV14 (Dazhui) and GV2 (Yaoshu) treat warm malaria and jie malaria (matching Mingtang). BL11 (Dashu) treats malaria, stiff neck unable to flex or extend, headache, and rigors. SI5 (Qiangu), Feng

💡 涉及穴位：内庭 ST44、厉兑 ST45、公孙 SP4、京骨 BL64、神门 HT7、合谷 LI4、阳溪 LI5、后溪 SI3、阳池 TE4、阴都 KI19、天枢 ST25、列缺 LU7、少商 LU11、经渠 LU8、大椎 GV14、腰俞 GV2、大杼 BL11、前谷 SI2（‘风’疑为风池 GB20或风府 GV16，接下段）。‘鼓颔’指寒战严重时上下颌叩击发声。‘纵唇口’指口唇弛缓、开合失常，可见于神志或面神经/肌肉状态异常。中医认为手太阴、手阳明、足阳明等经脉与外感热病、胸腹气机关系密切；现代医学中高热寒战、口干咽痛、腹胀或意识状态改变（如烦躁、易惊）反映全身炎症反应或中枢神经系统受影响。临床与考试中合谷 LI4、列缺 LU7、大椎 GV14、神门 HT7、天枢 ST25均为核心常用穴。

池神道見頭痛百會治痎瘧上星治痎瘧振寒熱汗不出偏厯主風瘧汗不出千少澤明云頭痛銅云寒熱復溜崑崙主瘧寒汗不出衝陽主瘧先寒洗淅甚久而熱熱去汗出然谷崑崙主瘧多汗甲云主瘧多汗腰痛不可俛仰目如脱項如㧞列缺後谿少澤前谷主瘧寒熱太泉太谿經渠主瘧欬逆心悶不得卧寒熱大陵腕骨陽谷少衝主乍寒乍熱瘧天樞主瘧振寒熱盛狂言太鍾主瘧多寒少熱甲云瘧悶嘔甚熱多寒少欲閉户而處寒厥足熱商丘主寒瘧腹痛少海主瘧背振寒甲云項痛引肘掖腰痛少腹四支不舉陽谿主瘧甚苦寒欬嘔沫厲兊内庭主瘧不嗜食惡寒少商主瘧振慄鼓頷商丘神庭上星百會完骨風池神道掖門前谷光明至隂大杼主痎瘧熱隂都少海商陽三閒中渚主瘧身熱列缺主瘧甚熱陽谷主瘧脇痛不得息俠谿主瘧足痛衝陽束骨主瘧從脚胻起陽谷主瘧脇痛不得息飛揚主狂瘧頭3392痛痙反折温溜主瘧靣赤腫天井主瘧食時發心痛悲傷不樂天府主瘧病譩譆支正小海主風瘧三里陷谷俠谿飛揚主痎瘧少氣

池（疑接上文‘风池’）、神道见于头痛。百会主治痎疟。上星主治痎疟、寒战发热而无汗。偏历主治风疟无汗（《千金》记载）。少泽，《明堂》说治头痛，《铜人》说治寒热。复溜、昆仑主治疟疾怕冷无汗。冲阳主治疟疾先恶寒、寒意如水洒洗般持续很久才发热，热退后出汗。然谷、昆仑主治疟疾多汗；《甲乙经》说主治疟疾多汗、腰痛不能弯腰仰身、眼睛像要脱出、后颈像要拔起一样牵拉。列缺、后溪、少泽、前谷主治疟疾寒热。太渊（原文作‘太泉’）、太溪、经渠主治疟疾咳逆、心闷不能平卧、寒热。大陵、腕骨、阳谷、少冲主治忽冷忽热（乍寒乍热）的疟疾。天枢主治疟疾寒战、高热、狂言。大钟（原文作‘太钟’）主治疟疾多寒少热；《甲乙经》说疟疾心闷呕吐很重，或热多寒少想关门独处，寒厥而足心热。商丘主治寒疟、腹痛。少海主治疟疾背部寒战；《甲乙经》说项痛牵引肘腋、腰痛、少腹及四肢不能举动。阳溪主治疟疾极度怕冷、咳嗽吐白沫。厉兑、内庭主治疟疾不想吃东西、恶寒。少商主治疟疾寒战发抖、上下颌叩击。商丘、神庭、上星、百会、完骨、风池、神道、掖门（即液门）、前谷、光明、至阴、大杼主治痎疟发热。阴都、少海、商阳、三间、中渚主治疟疾身热。列缺主治疟疾高热（甚热）。阳谷主治疟疾胁痛不能喘息。侠溪主治疟疾足痛。冲阳、束骨主治疟疾从脚胫部发动向上。阳谷主治疟疾胁痛不能喘息。飞扬主治狂疟、头眩痛（原文‘头3392痛’，3392为底本缺字，当为‘眩’）、抽搐角弓反张（痉反折）。温溜主治疟疾面赤肿。天井主治疟疾在进食时发作、心痛、悲伤不乐。天府主治疟疾病症。譩譆、支正、小海主治风疟。足三里（原文作‘三里’）、陷谷、侠溪、飞扬主治痎疟少气。

Chi (continuing 'Fengchi', GB20) and GV11 (Shendao) appear under headache. GV20 (Baihui) treats jie malaria. GV23 (Shangxing) treats jie malaria with rigors, fever, and absence of sweating. LI6 (Pianli) treats wind malaria without sweating (per Qianjin). SI1 (Shaoze) is noted in Mingtang for headache and in Tongren for chills and fever. KI7 (Fuliu) and BL60 (Kunlun) treat malaria with chills and no sweating. ST42 (Chongyang) treats malaria starting with prolonged drenching/washing chills before fever, followed by sweating as fever subsides. KI2 (Rangu) and BL60 (Kunlun) treat malaria with excessive sweating; Jiayi Jing adds excessive sweating, lower back pain unable to flex or extend, eyes feeling as if popping out, and neck feeling as if pulled out. LU7 (Lieque), SI3 (Houxi), SI1 (Shaoze), and SI2 (Qiangu) treat malaria with chills and fever. LU9 (Taiyuan, written 'Taiquan'), KI3 (Taixi), and LU8 (Jingqu) treat malaria with hiccup/rebellious qi, chest oppression unable to lie flat, and chills and fever. PC7 (Daling), SI4 (Wangu), SI5 (Yanggu—note: Yanggu is SI5, here listed alongside Wangu), and HT9 (Shaochong) treat malaria with alternating/sudden chills and fever. ST25 (Tianshu) treats malaria with rigors, high fever, and delirious speech. KI4 (Dazhong, written 'Taizhong') treats malaria with much chill and little fever; Jiayi Jing notes severe chest oppression and vomiting, or much fever and little chill wanting to close the door and stay alone, with cold extremities/cold reversal and hot feet. SP5 (Shangqiu) treats cold malaria and abdominal pain. HT3 (Shaohai) treats malaria with back rigors; Jiayi Jing adds neck pain pulling to elbow and axilla, low back pain, and inability to raise lower abdomen and limbs. LI5 (Yangxi) treats malaria with severe aversion to cold, cough, and foamy sputum. ST45 (Lidui) and ST44 (Neiting) treat malaria with poor appetite and aversion to cold. LU11 (Shaoshang) treats malaria with shivering/trembling and clattering jaws. SP5 (Shangqiu), GV24 (Shenting), GV23 (Shangxing), GV20 (Baihui), GB12 (Wangu), GB20 (Fengchi), GV11 (Shendao), TE2 (Yemen), SI2 (Qiangu), GB37 (Guangming), BL67 (Zhiyin), and BL11 (Dazhui) treat fever in jie malaria. KI19 (Yindu), HT3 (Shaohai), LI1 (Shangyang), LI3 (Sanjian), and TE3 (Zhongzhu) treat malaria with body fever. LU7 (Lieque) treats malaria with very high fever. SI5 (Yanggu) treats malaria with hypochondriac/lateral chest pain and inability to breathe easily. GB43 (Xiaxi) treats malaria with foot pain. ST42 (Chongyang) and BL65 (Shugu) treat malaria rising from the lower leg/shin (jiaoxing). SI5 (Yanggu) treats malaria with lateral chest pain and inability to breathe easily (repeated in text). BL58 (Feiyang) treats manic malaria ('kuang nue'), dizziness and headache, and tetanic opisthotonos/spasm (jing fanzhe). LI7 (Wenliu) treats malaria with red facial swelling. TE10 (Tianjing) treats malaria attacking around mealtime, heart pain, and grief/unhappiness. LU3 (Tianfu) treats malarial illness. BL45 (Yixi), SI7 (Zhizheng), and HT3 (Shaohai) treat wind malaria. ST36 (Zusanli, written 'Sanli'), ST43 (Xiangu), GB43 (Xiaxi), and BL58 (Feiyang) treat jie malaria with shortness of breath.

💡 涉及穴位：风池 GB20、神道 GV11、百会 GV20、上星 GV23、偏历 LI6、少泽 SI1、复溜 KI7、昆仑 BL60、冲阳 ST42、然谷 KI2、列缺 LU7、后溪 SI3、前谷 SI2、太渊 LU9（‘太泉’即太渊）、太溪 KI3、经渠 LU8、大陵 PC7、腕骨 SI4、阳谷 SI5、少冲 HT9、天枢 ST25、大钟 KI4（‘太钟’即大钟）、商丘 SP5、少海 HT3、阳溪 LI5、厉兑 ST45、内庭 ST44、少商 LU11、神庭 GV24、完骨 GB12、掖门即液门 TE2、光明 GB37、至阴 BL67、大杼 BL11、阴都 KI19、商阳 LI1、三间 LI3、中渚 TE3、侠溪 GB43、束骨 BL65、飞扬 BL58、温溜 LI7、天井 TE10、天府 LU3、譩譆 BL45、支正 SI7、足三里 ST36、陷谷 ST43。‘狂疟’‘痉反折’指高热时伴发精神躁狂或抽搐角弓反张，现代医学中需警惕高热惊厥、中枢神经系统感染或严重代谢紊乱。‘无汗’与‘多汗’对应体温调节的不同阶段（寒战期血管收缩无汗、退热期血管扩张出汗）。古籍存在传抄重复（如‘阳谷主疟胁痛不得息’出现两次）与错别字（‘太泉’‘太钟’‘掖门’），研读时应互参《内经》《甲乙经》《铜人》校勘。

大附子一枚炮末薑兩半取自然汁丸如小豆大每十五丸空心熱酒吞下老少加減川客治瘧只二三服皆愈云兼治脾胃愈於薑附湯故附此

用大附子一枚炮制后研成细末，用生姜一两半捣取自然汁，和药做成小豆大小的丸子，每次服十五丸，空腹时用热酒送下，老人和小孩根据情况增减剂量。川客用此方治疗疟疾，只服两三剂就都痊愈了。有人说它还能兼治脾胃病，效果胜过姜附汤，所以附载在这里。

Take one large aconite lateral root (fuzi), prepare/roast it and grind it to a fine powder; take 1.5 liang of fresh ginger and express its natural juice; form pills the size of a small adzuki bean, taking 15 pills each time on an empty stomach with warm wine, adjusting the dose for the elderly and children. Chuanke used this formula to treat malaria, and patients all recovered after just two or three doses. It is said to also treat spleen-stomach conditions with results superior to Jiangfu Tang (Ginger and Aconite Decoction), so it is appended here.

💡 本段为内服药方附记，体现王执中‘针药并重’及脾胃寒湿/阳虚截疟思路。附子辛热大毒，走而不守，炮制后温阳散寒，配生姜汁宣散温中。现代医学高度警示：附子（含乌头碱类生物碱）具有显著心脏毒性与神经毒性，炮制不当、煎煮时间不足或剂量过大极易引起心律失常甚至致命中毒，且‘热酒送服’与‘老少加减’在现代临床安全上风险极高，绝不可自行配服或随意用于儿童、老年人及心律失常患者。

脾疼餘見心腹痛

关于‘脾疼’（脾区疼痛），其余相关内容详见‘心腹痛’门。

For 'spleen pain' (pain in the spleen region), see further details under 'heart and abdominal pain' (epigastric and abdominal pain).

💡 篇目互见注记。古代‘脾疼’多指左上腹或中上腹偏左部位的疼痛、胀痛，常与脾胃气滞、寒凝或饮食积滞相关；其余详目见他卷‘心腹痛’。

府舎治疝癖脾中急痛循脅上下搶心腹滿積聚厥氣兩乳銅商丘治脾虚令人不樂見腸鳴千見吐三隂交治脾病身重見腹脹

府舍穴主治疝气积癖、脾部急痛并沿着胁肋上下窜痛冲心、腹胀、积聚、厥逆之气上冲及两乳胀痛（《铜人》记载）。商丘穴主治脾虚导致的闷闷不乐，见于肠鸣症，《千金》见于呕吐症。三阴交穴主治脾病引起的身体沉重，见于腹胀症。

SP13 (Fushe) treats hernia and abdominal masses/accumulations (shanpi), acute pain in the spleen region ascending and descending along the lateral chest/hypochondrium and thrusting toward the heart, abdominal fullness, accumulations, counterflow qi (jueqi), and pain/distension of both breasts (per Tongren). SP5 (Shangqiu) treats unhappiness/low mood due to spleen deficiency, appearing under borborygmus (intestinal rumbling) and in Qianjin under vomiting. SP6 (Sanyinjiao) treats heaviness of the body in spleen disorders and appears under abdominal distension.

💡 涉及穴位：府舍 SP13、商丘 SP5、三阴交 SP6。府舍为足太阴脾经与阴维脉交会穴，传统主治下腹、胁肋及疝瘕积聚；商丘为脾经经穴，能健脾除湿、调畅情志（脾虚则思虑多、不乐）；三阴交为足三阴经交会穴，健脾利湿、通调气血。现代医学中左上腹及胁肋窜痛需注意胃、胰、脾或结肠病变及腹壁神经痛；肠鸣、腹胀常见于胃肠动力或菌群变化；三阴交、商丘等穴在临床上常用于改善腹胀、消化不良及乏力感。

予嘗久患脾疼服治脾藥反膨脹不得已依耆域方用麵裹火炮蓬莪茂末水與酒醋煎服立愈已而告人人亦云高良薑末米飲調服亦作效後鄭敎授傳一方云草果延胡索苓脂并没藥酒調三兩錢一似手拈却草果子五苓脂四味等分為末此亦平穩藥也有此疾者宜服之或不吐不瀉心中疼甚日輕夜甚者用乾鹽梅并茶煎服神效若灸者宜上管中管下管脾俞三隂交等穴

我曾经长期患脾痛，服用治脾的药物反而觉得胀满。不得已依照耆婆（耆域）的方子，用面粉包裹在火中炮制蓬莪茂，研末后用水、酒和醋同煎服用，立刻痊愈。事后我把方法告诉别人，有人也说用高良姜末加米汤调服同样有效。后来郑教授传授一方：是一首歌诀：“草果延胡索，灵脂并没药，酒调三两钱，一似手拈却”——用草果、延胡索、五灵脂、没药，酒调服二三钱，疼痛就像用手拈掉一样（这就是“手拈散”）；草果仁、五灵脂等四味等分研末。这也是一剂较为平和的药，有这种病的人适宜服用。如果既不吐又不泻，但心窝处疼痛很剧烈，白天轻、夜间重的，用干盐梅配茶叶煎服，有神效。如果要用艾灸治疗，适宜选用上脘（原文作‘上管’）、中脘（原文作‘中管’）、下脘（原文作‘下管’）、脾俞、三阴交等穴位。

I once suffered from chronic spleen pain, and taking spleen-tonifying medicines instead caused bloating/distension. Having no other choice, following Jivaka's (Qiyu's) formula, I wrapped zedoary rhizome (peng'ezhu/curcuma) in dough, roasted it in fire, ground it to powder, and decocted it with water, wine, and vinegar; it cured me immediately. Afterward I told others, and someone also reported that taking galangal rhizome powder (gaoliangjiang) mixed with rice gruel was effective. Later Professor Zheng passed down a formula using tsaoko fruit (caoguo), corydalis rhizome (yanhusuo), trogopterus dung (wulingzhi, written 'lingzhi'), and myrrh, in a jingle: take two or three qian in wine and the pain goes as if plucked away by hand (hence the name Shounian San, 'Hand-Plucking Powder'); the four ingredients are ground in equal parts; this is also a relatively gentle remedy, suitable for those with this illness. If there is neither vomiting nor diarrhea, yet pain in the heart/epigastric region is severe and lighter by day but worse at night, decocting dried salted plum (gan yanmei) with tea has miraculous effect. For moxibustion, suitable points include CV13 (Shangwan, written 'Shangguan'), CV12 (Zhongwan, written 'Zhongguan'), CV10 (Xiawan, written 'Xiaguan'), BL20 (Pishu), and SP6 (Sanyinjiao).

💡 涉及穴位：上脘 CV13、中脘 CV12、下脘 CV10、脾俞 BL20、三阴交 SP6（‘管’即‘脘’）。本段展示了中医‘理气活血止痛’与‘温中散寒’思路：补益药反胀多因气滞或寒凝，改用蓬莪茂、高良姜、延胡索、没药行气活血、温中散寒止痛。‘苓脂’即五灵脂，需注意“人参畏五灵脂”属传统“十九畏”配伍禁忌；延胡索含延胡索乙素，有镇痛作用；草果、高良姜芳香温燥。现代医学中上腹痛伴夜间加重需警惕消化性溃疡、胆胰疾病或胃食管反流等器质性病变，不可仅凭单方或偏方延误检查与规范治疗；上脘、中脘、下脘、足三里/三阴交等在功能性消化不良或慢性腹痛调理中可作辅助。

鍼灸資生經卷三

《针灸资生经》卷三

Zhenjiu Zisheng Jing (Classic of Nourishing Life with Acupuncture and Moxibustion), Volume 3

💡 书名与卷次题署。南宋王执中编撰《针灸资生经》，广搜《素问》《灵枢》《难经》《甲乙经》《千金》《外台》《铜人》及宋代医家验案，以临床实用和灸法特色著称。

Study notes and general health information, not a diagnosis or a treatment plan. Nothing here replaces a doctor, pharmacist or licensed TCM practitioner, and no individual treatment advice is given. Unwell: see a doctor or call 8-1-1 in BC. Emergency: 911.这里是学习笔记和一般健康资料，不是诊断，也不是治疗方案；不替代医生、药剂师或持照中医师，也不提供个人诊疗建议。身体不适请看医生，BC 可拨 8-1-1；紧急情况拨 911。
